Chapter 2851: Flood Tide Legion!

“Idon’t like the term ‘giant devils’. It is the history, wisdom, and legacy of the endless civilizations from the outside world, and even their enthusiastic invitation to explore the outside world.

Lu Qingchen frowned. “However, I understand what you mean. Please continue.”

“As you said, when a civilization thinks that it has reached the peak of the universe, is there any goal that is more exciting than exploring the boundless multiverse in the space zones?

“In the tens of thousands of years after the ‘Fuxi System’ was created and all the resources and data of the universe were combined, almost every individual of the Pangu civilization dedicated all their passion to the sacred mission of ‘crossing the black wall and exploring the outer domain’.

“Countless people are trying to explore the essence of the black wall and find a way through it.

“Countless people emptied their worlds and dedicated all their resources, trying to build an unprecedented, brilliant super fleet.

“All the stars in the universe were eclipsed by the surging exhaust flames of this glorious fleet. Countless generations, from birth to death, spent their lives in incomplete starships. The expedition fleet simply became synonymous with the supreme government of the Pangu Civilization. Countless poets

sang praises of it, and countless artists painted its brilliance in various forms. Almost all the children were instilled with the belief that ‘the fleet is number one’ from the moment they were born. They were proud to be a member of the expedition army, even if the expedition army would not set off until

thousands of years later.

“Just like that, the passion of the entire civilization burned for tens of thousands of years. The ‘Fuxi System’ finally calculated the best route to pass through the black wall. The expedition fleet, which contained the essence of the civilization, endless resources, and the hope of countless generations, was

gathered, too. The new generation of warriors with the firmest will, the clearest mind, and the strongest combat ability of the Pangu civilization broke through the black wall and explored the outer domain!

The picture of the ‘primeval expedition army’ appeared in the thousands of pictures around Fuxi.

It was a torrent of steel made of millions of advanced and unimaginably powerful starships.

Compared to them, the super arsenals of human beings right now were nothing but feeble sampans. Even the star fortresses modified from asteroids were the most common units that could be found everywhere in the fleet.

There was no telling whether or not Fuxi had exaggerated the strength and the number of the expedition fleet on purpose, but if its purpose was to intimidate Lu Qingchen, it was undoubtedly a success. Even Li Yao was amazed by the invincibility of the expedition fleet.

“Did you make it?”

Although he knew the answer, Lu Qingchen could not help but ask, “Did you really pass through the black wall and explore the truth of the outside world? What is it exactly? What is beyond the universe?”

The files are missing. Many details have been lost over the course of hundreds of thousands of years. I can’t describe the details of the outside world to you, but I do know that the brilliant expedition army built by the Pangu Civilization over the past tens of thousands of years, after going through

countless dangers and finally passing through the black wall, encountered an indescribable horror that seemed to be able to destroy everything.

Fuxi paused for a moment. When he was talking about the outside of the black wall, even the cluster of light spots that constituted him was shaking and dispersing like frightened fireflies. “It is a darkness and evil beyond anyone’s imagination and description. Anyone who looks at it will go mad and

collapse. A long speech in any language is not enough to describe one ten-thousandth of its real strength. Any magical equipment that tries to scan it will explode within one microsecond. Even if it is trying to paint its shape on a painting, the painting will bum furiously the moment the picture is

completed, and the painter will carve out his eyes and all his sensory organs and tear apart his optic nerves in extreme fear.

“No. The expedition army of the Pangu Civilization did not even get in touch with the master of the terrifying force. They only got in touch with its servants, only to lose seventy to eighty percent of their strength in an inexplicable encounter. They were almost crushed.

“After they learned more about it, they realized that the expedition army was quite lucky. If they had been in direct contact with the master of the terrifying force, not only would they have been annihilated instantly, the home behind them, the mother universe of the Pangu Civilization, would have been

swallowed by the indescribable force like a rolling ocean.

A servant of terrifying strength?

Lu Qingchen frowned. “What is that?”

The clusters of light spots that constituted the Fuxis wriggled and changed into a complicated, three-dimensional rune that contained endless information.

‘What was weird was that it was not even a ‘3D’ rune. It was more like a ‘four-dimensional flower’ that had been created by the Fuxi Clan.

‘The four-dimensional flower blossomed in front of Lu Qingchen, giving him a deep understanding of the horror, mystery, and desperation faced by the expedition army of the Pangu Civilization.

Li Yao read a lot of data from the spreading ripples of the four-dimensional flower and translated the information contained in the rune with what he knew.

“Flood Tide Legion?”

It was exactly what Fuxi meant to Lu Qingchen.

The so-called ‘Flood Tide Legion’ was not the real flood, but the remnants of many civilizations that had collapsed because of the scourge of the flood and lost their fighting will. They had surrendered to the supreme power of the flood in order to survive.

For their own survival, the scumbags of the civilization would act as scouts and vanguard officers for the flood tide. They would wander in the multiverse, hunting down the innocent prey and sending them to the flood tide as sacrifices to the unspeakable power.

Of course, if they encountered a tough nut to crack, they would send the coordinates of the multiverse to the depths of the flood so that the flood would come and finish the enemy in person.

If the tides were the ferocious beasts that devoured the multiverse, then the Flood Tide Legion was the fleas on the bodies of the ferocious beasts.

It was the conclusion that Fu Xi came up with after countless years of collecting information from various sources.

In the end, Li Yao picked up a huge bargain and gradually sorted out his thoughts.

“Lunderstand now. The glorious expedition army that the Pangu civilization was so proud of failed on their first expedition to the space zones and was almost wiped out by the Flood Tide Legion. Therefore, you were scared out of your wits and lost all your courage and fighting will.

Lu Qingchen sneered, “He fled to his hometown with his tail between his legs and sealed the entire home universe from then on. He even castrated his seven emotions, six desires, curiosity, and ambition, ready to be a coward for the rest of his life!

“The conclusion is correct, but the process is completely wrong.

Fuxi was not angry at all. However, there was sorrow in the blinking light spots. “At that time, it was the golden era of the Pangu civilization. It was as unstoppable as the rising sun. Every individual was determined and resolute. How could they be swayed by the outcome of a battle?

“Besides, we won the battle against the ‘Flood Tide Legion’. Although we paid a high price, we annihilated all the enemies and even retrieved a lot of debris, scraps, and advanced technology from the ‘Flood Tide Legion’!

“You won?”

Lu Qingchen was dazed for a moment. He nodded and said, “That’s right. If the ‘Flood Tide Legion’ wins, naturally, they will pursue the victory and conquer our universe. Then, there will be no Pangu civilization or human civilization in the future.

“Therefore, not only did you annihilate the ‘Flood Tide Legion’, you also blocked the battlefield so that the enemy could not inform the ‘Flood Tide Legion’ of any intelligence about you.

“Ah, an expedition fleet that had just left their home universe encountered a mysterious enemy whose capability was far higher than their own in the vast sea of stars. With their fearlessness and determination, they paid an unimaginable price and finally defeated the strong with the weak. It was truly a

thrilling and blood-boiling victory!

“However, as a result, I don’t understand why the expedition army was so timid and dispirited. Were you scared by the brutal sacrifices?

“Not exactly. The Pangu civilization is not as naive and fragile as you think. They were prepared for the darkness and danger of the space zones a long time ago. If they were scared of sacrifices, they would not have launched such an expedition at all.

“Although we suffered heavy losses, we also retrieved a lot of advanced trophies,” Fuxi said. “The technology contained in many trophies has been fully analyzed. The level of the Pangu civilization has been greatly improved.

“Many of the captives even hid the mysteries of other universes, which could potentially expand the living space of the Pangu civilization by dozens of times!

“In the face of such abundant trophies, the remaining soldiers of the expedition army had their eyes burning red. Who would care about temporary sacrifices?

“Besides, at that time, they were far from understanding the true horror of the ‘Flood Tide’. They thought that the ‘Flood Tide Legion’ was the true form of the Flood Tide and mistook the fleas for ferocious beasts.

“The Flood Tide Legion is strong, but the technological gap between the two sides is not so large that it is incomprehensible or invincible. In fact, many commanders of the expedition army who survived felt that, although the technology of the Flood Tide Legion is advanced, their tactics are too rigid, and

they are not as smart as the Pangu Civilization.

“In other words, if they can bring the debris of the Flood Tide Legion back to their home universe and seclude themselves for thousands of years to digest the debris, they will be able to make a comeback. With the wisdom of the Pangu Civilization and the technology of the Flood Tide Legion, they will be

able to sweep across the entire universe.

“Therefore, although the remaining soldiers of the expedition army suffered heavy losses, their morale was not low. On the contrary, they were quite excited. From the commanders to the soldiers at the bottom level, they all thought that the trip was worthwhile and that they could fight again in the

future.

“However, a discovery on the way back crushed everyone’s innocence, pride, and hope, making them realize the darkness, cruelty, and horror of the multiverse and turning them into… emotionless, walking dead!

Chapter 2852: Builder Of The Black Screen

“Why are you being so mysterious?”

Lu Qingchen sneered, “I don’t believe that they could grit their teeth through a fierce battle where 90% of their soldiers were killed. What else could make the iron-blood warriors collapse instantly? What did they find exactly?”

“A hub.”

“Or rather, a switch,” Fuxi said.

“A… switch?”

Lu Qingchen was confused. “The control hub and the switch?”

“The hub that controls the black wall.

“The switch to turn on and off the black wall instantly,” Fuxi said. “If so, it should not be called ‘black wall’ but ‘black domain’ or ‘black curtain’. It is a dark barrier that can be turned on and off at will.

His words froze Lu Qingchen’s soul like a bucket of liquid nitrogen.

Li Yao’s soul was the same. His every thought was frozen.

‘They were all overwhelmed by confusion and shock. It took them a long time to digest the words of the Fuxi.

“What—what did you say?”

Lu Qingchen’s soul was in a mess, with tiny swirls appearing one after another. “Are you suggesting that the dark vacuum beyond our universe is not natural but man-made?

“Somebody—someone extinguished the brilliance of billions of stars on purpose and shattered all the planets and celestial bodies in the universe, turning the space across billions of lightyears into a dark mist.

“How is it possible? How is it possible? The scale of the ‘black wall’ is much larger than the diameter of the universe where the three thousand Sectors are located. Nobody can estimate how vast it is at all. For thousands of years, countless civilizations, forces, and super experts have tried to cross the black

wall and get out of the universe, only to come back empty-handed or even defeated!

“We used to think that the black wall was an impassable chasm. If it were an astronomical phenomenon generated by a certain universe, then—then it would be understandable. But you are saying that the black wall is man-made and a dark barrier controlled by a certain force? That’s—that’s impossible.

It’s absolutely impossible!

“Black wall, black screen, or black domain—you can call this damn thing whatever you want. If it was really man-made, how could its creator extinguish billions of stars, block countless celestial bodies, and crush billions of planets in order to create such an empty death zone?”

The more Lu Qingchen spoke, the more he read. In the end, he roared hysterically.

He was trying to hide his shock and panic by roaring crazily.

Li Yao felt the same. He wanted to roar at the sky and vent the feeling of insignificance in his heart that could not be described with words.

“The details are like flying sand in the wind. They’ve all faded away after hundreds of thousands of years. Maybe I can recover some of the files after I find more computational and storage units.

“Tonly know that the technology to build a dark barrier is not as complicated as you think,” Fuxi said calmly. “We don’t need to extinguish billions of stars and crush planets that are hundreds of times larger. We just need to block the light, heat, radiation, and high-energy particle streams that they send

out in a certain way and block all the information and data.

“Tt means that the star systems and other celestial bodies that should exist in certain coordinates are still in their original trajectories. Their information has been ‘blocked’ so that the observers at a lower level or at a long distance cannot find them.

“Of course, you were right. Even if the billions of stars did not need to be shattered, the ‘dark block’ on such a large scale still required unimaginably advanced technology. Not just the human beings today, even the Pangu civilization at that time could not have imagined it.

“At that time, the Pangu civilization had already grasped the technology to utilize the energy of the stars efficiently. They could build large-scale energy bases near the stars, even enclosing part of the star and forming the ‘Kuafu Sphere’. They also had the ability to detonate the star and destroy the entire

galaxy, which was known as the ‘Houyi Arrow’.

“However, such technology is even worse than ‘drilling wood for fire’ when compared to the technology of ‘black screen builders’. Both ‘Kuafu’s Ball’ and ‘Houyi’s Arrow are as childish as toys for three-year-olds.

“By the most optimistic estimation, the gap between the peak technology of the Pangu civilization and the technology of the schemer is at least five million years to infinity. In other words, with the best luck, the most abundant resources, and everyone working together, it will take at least another five

million years for the civilization to be as advanced as the schemer.

“But most of the time, the progress of a civilization is not something that can be achieved with the accumulation of time. In fact, the Pangu civilization is already established and mature. It has missed the best ‘growth and development period’. It is almost impossible for it to reach the level of the architect

of the black curtain.

“It’s like an expert reaching the Building Foundation Stage in their prime when they are 120 years old. It proves that their talent is second only to that of the Building Foundation Stage. No matter how many resources and adventures they are given, it will be difficult for them to reach the Divinity

Transformation Stage in their entire life… Do you understand what I’m saying?”

“L… understand. The sapling has been growing crooked since childhood. No matter how much fertilizer and watering is done, it is impossible for it to grow into a towering tree.”

Lu Qingchen struggled to say, “Faced with such a terrifying gap between civilizations, it’s no wonder that the top experts of the Pangu civilization were all scared out of their wits. But who was the ‘constructioner of the black curtain’ exactly? Why did they block the starlight and the radiation and create

such a vast black barrier?

“A certain prehistoric civilization.”

“The Pangu civilization was not the first batch of intelligent creatures in our universe,” Fuxi sai

that wreathed our universe. It is the information recorded on the ‘core’ and the ‘switch’.’

“In the billions of years before the primeval era, there were still endless intelligent creatures and powerful civilizations that rose and fell. One of the most powerful prehistoric civilizations built a dark barrier

“Unbelievable.”

Lu Qingchen asked, “Does the Pangu civilization have such a clear understanding about the prehistoric civilization?”

“Of course.”

“If we compress the history of our universe into one short year, the Pangu civilization will only show up at dawn on the last day of the 365 days, and the civilization of mankind will only show up at noon or dusk on this day. However, in the past 364 days, how many lives have emerged and how many

brilliant civilizations have been created? Who knows?

“The civilization of mankind absorbed nutrients from the relics of the Pangu civilization, and the Pangu civilization received enlightenment from the relics of the prehistoric civilization. That is the true meaning of the legacy of civilization.

“Does that mean that the Pangu Civilization has excavated the relics of some prehistoric civilizations?”

Lu Qingchen replied quickly, “Then you should’ve known the existence and formidability of the prehistoric civilization long ago. It is indeed shocking that they were so strong, but it is not enough to make them collapse, is it?”

“Yes. The existence of the dark barrier alone is not enough to collapse all the experts of the Pangu Civilization.

“But why don’t you think about it carefully? Why did the prehistoric civilization build the dark barrier?”

Lu Qingchen pondered for a moment. The shivering of his soul was becoming more and more obvious. “Hide. They are trying to hide themselves. They are hiding from something or an enemy that is too dangerous to resist. Therefore, they are trying to hide themselves!

“That’s right.”

“Then why don’t you guess what they are scared of and what they are hiding from?”

“The Flood Tide.”

Lu Qingchen’s soul was even colder than liquid nitrogen. “The prehistoric civilizations were so scared of the tide that they would rather spend endless resources to erase the radiation and information of all the stars around them and seal themselves and their children and grandchildren billions of years

later in the dark barrier. They did not dare to go out and fight the tide because they knew that they had no chance of winning at all!

“Wait. There’s a problem here. In a place where a lot of stars and celestial bodies are supposed to exist, if a black mist is created to cover everything, won’t a… weird black spot appear? Will the observers outside not notice it?”

“Think of the dark barrier as a thick eggshell. The patterns inside and outside of the eggshell are completely different.

“If you look from the inside, you will find that the dark barrier is a space zone with few resources. But if you look from the outside, you will naturally see something else. Maybe you will be able to fake a world that is barren, boring, and has no value for exploration or hunting, and the hunters will stop

thinking about coming here.

“Allin all, the dark barrier is not a simple black spot. Other than concealing itself, it can also block and filter. On one hand, it can block the newborn civilizations who don’t know the immensity of heaven and earth in our universe from rushing out and committing suicide. On the other hand, it can block

and filter the harmful information transmitted from the outside world, just like the atmosphere can block the ultraviolet rays.

“From this, it can be seen how much thought was put into the construction of the black curtain for the newborn civilization billions of years later.

“However, that was not the reason for desperation.

“The reason for the desperation is that even such a strong prehistoric civilization could not resist the flood at all and could only bury their head in the sand. Then, how strong must the real flood be?

“Now, do you understand?

“Only when the gap between the strength of the two parties is reduced to a certain extent will there be desperation and fear. Ants will never be desperate and fearful of dinosaurs because the two parties are not on the same level at all. Ants cannot understand the strength of dinosaurs at all. Even if they are

stomped to death by dinosaurs, they will die in confusion and fearlessness.

“Only when some sort of reference object appears, some sort of reference object that the ant can understand, will it be able to understand a tiny bit of the strength and tyranny of dinosaurs—only then will it know how vast, how dangerous, and how callous this world is. Only then will it know that it will

never be able to become the master of this world.. Its destiny is to be trampled under the feet of all kinds of powerful existences time and time again!”

Chapter 2853: : Lu Qingchen’S Brilliance!

As the Fuxi roared, the clusters of light spots that constituted its ‘body’ turned into lackluster black spots, too, which danced around Lu Qingchen’s soul like countless black holes the size of needles. Black mist was released, as if it were going to envelop and swallow his soul and seal him in a specific, tiny

‘dark barrier’.

“Do you know what the deepest desperation is? If you encounter the strongest enemy the moment you leave your hometown, and you are defeated or even wiped out by the enemy, it is not the deepest desperation. You will only be stomped to death by the dinosaurs.

Fuxi’s voice was low and full of sorrow and pity. “However, after exhausting all his strength, burning all his wisdom and courage, and making countless sacrifices, you finally defeated an unprecedented enemy. When the joy of victory and your hope for the future reached their peak, you suddenly realized

that your so-called ‘victory’ had merely shaken off a hair of the enemy, and the enemy had not discovered you at all. The feeling of falling from the clouds into an endless abyss was the deepest desperation.

“Think about it. Think about it carefully. Put yourself in the role of the commander-in-chief of the expedition army of the Pangu Civilization. You are leading the strongest fleet that was established after a hundred thousand years of civilization and the resources of three thousand Sectors. You are

commanding the top experts of the thirteen carbon-based civilizations. You are embarking on an unknown journey with the earnest hope of your hometown and the multiverse.

“The difficulty of crossing the black wall goes without saying. The vastness and cruelty of the multiverse are within your expectations, too. Even the unexpected encounter with the Flood Tide Legion did not destroy your mind, because you discovered that, although the Flood Tide Fleet was strong, it was

not invincible.

“Finally, after hot-bloodedness, courage, wisdom, indomitable will, and endless sacrifices, you have finally secured a world-shaking victory. It is a battle that will forever be recorded in the history books. You feel that all the soldiers under your command have performed to the best of their abilities. Even

your own commanding skills have reached the peak of perfection and cannot be duplicated!

“Although the losses were heavy, the harvests were even more bountiful. Countless people sacrificed themselves in the cold darkness, but they left the brightest hope for the survivors and encouraged them to continue marching forward. You thought that you had seen clearly the gap between you and the

strongest Cultivators of the multiverse, and that you had seen clearly the direction of evolution despite the twists and turns ahead of you.

“It is like a touching legend that has just begun. The ruler of the multiverse will take the first solid step in the future, right?

“Unfortunately, it is not.

“On your way back, you discovered the switches and last words left by the ‘black curtain maker’ and leamed one billionth of the truth of the universe.

“When you realize that even such an unbelievably powerful prehistoric civilization as the ‘black curtain makers’ can only be cowards, the entire world will collapse before your eyes.

“There is a gap of ten thousand times between you and the architect of the black curtain, and there is a gap of ten thousand times between the architect of the black curtain and the real flood. Even the architect of the black curtain did not dare to fight the flood to the death. Isn’t everything you and your

fearless compatriots have done a joke?

“The cause that you’ve been fighting for your entire life, or even your entire civilization, for a hundred thousand years, has collapsed. It’s meaningless. Everything is meaningless. Right now, you already know that hundreds of civilizations hundreds of times stronger than yours have tried the same thing

before your civilization was born. The only ‘right answer’ is to build a black screen and be a turtle that hides in its shell. Even if your civilization develops for another million years, it will only be in the phase where it wants to be a turtle but cannot.

“What is the significance of your existence? What is the significance of the Pangu civilization? What is the significance of the universe?

“Now that you’ve learned everything, do you have a second choice except to collapse?

Under the interrogation of Fuxi, Lu Qingchen’s soul dimmed, shook, and twisted again.

Like the brightness that was about to be bunt out in a storm, the brightness faded away with the scattering sparks.

Even Li Yao, who was eavesdropping, was greatly shocked and shaken. He was almost drowned in the vast universe of darkness and desperation.

For the human civilization today, the cutting-edge technology of the Pangu Civilization had been advanced for tens of thousands of years. At first glance, they looked like real deities.

In the eyes of the Pangu civilization, the makers of the black curtains were countless times more advanced than them. They were the ‘devils of the devils.

Even the deities did not dare to face the flood. They could only create a dark area to hide themselves, as if they were children who had been scared by a ghost story and were shivering in bed!

Then, what did the so-called flood mean for the civilization of mankind today?

If the flood was really related to Earth and the Vulture Plan demanded that he destroy the planet, could he really do it?

‘When faced with the resurrected Pangu Clan, Li Yao could still cheer himself up. “We are all carbon-based creatures. We are not scared of each other!”

However, when faced with the mysterious flood, or even the ‘black curtain maker’, Li Yao found it hard to boast about himself, because it was very likely that the enemy was of a higher level that even the three-dimensional carbon-based creatures could not understand.

On the other hand, Fuxi’s words had solved a mystery that had been bothering Li Yao for a long time.

It was the significance of the Ancient Sacred World.

In the primeval war hundreds of thousands of years ago, why did the Pangu Clan establish such a large dark nebula in the periphery of the Ancient Saint World?

In the beginning, many experts and scholars, including Long Yangjun, had pointed out that the Pangu Clan’s plan to treat the ancient sacred world as a ‘rejuvenation base had gone wrong during the implementation of their plan, which led to the extinction of all the Pangu Clan in the ancient sacred world.

That was why human beings had survived to this day.

However, Li Yao had always felt that such a theory was too strange. Many things did not make sense.

It was not until today that he realized that the Ancient Saint World was most likely a ‘reverse engineering test base’ where the Pangu civilization had chosen a certain space zone at the edge of the sea of stars to try to copy and analyze the technology of the ‘black market’ after they got the technology of the

‘black market’.

Therefore, the dark nebula around the Ancient Saint World was very similar to the black wall that sealed the entire Pangu Universe.

Of course, the ‘black curtain technology’ that the Pangu Clan dissected and reverse-developed was far less advanced than that of the ‘black curtain makers’. Therefore, the dark nebula around the Ancient Saint World was not entirely undetectable. It was merely thin and lackluster.

It also proved that there was more to the story than met the eye. At the very least, the existence of the ‘black curtain makers’ should be undeniable.

“shut up! Stop talking!”

While Li Yao was pondering, he suddenly heard Lu Qingchen’s furious roar. “Put away your tricks, Fuxi. I finally know why you are telling me stories from hundreds of thousands of years ago so hypocritically. Hehe. You are trying to trap me in the same desperate situation as the commander-in-chief of

the expedition army of the Pangu Civilization in such a way so that my soul defense line will collapse and you can swallow and me easily, aren’t you?

“Tm sorry. I won’t be fooled, nor will I collapse and succumb.

“Even if everything you said is the truth, even if the top predators beyond our imagination existed billions of years ago and billions of lightyears away, it is definitely not a reason for us to give up our feelings, curiosity, ambition, and hope.

“What’s wrong with ants? The evolution of countless planets has proved that the survival ability of ants is far better than that of dinosaurs. Countless seemingly powerful dinosaurs and reptiles went extinct during the catastrophes, but thousands of species of ants have survived to this day!

“Bacteria? So what? Can a civilization as strong as the Pangu civilization or the human civilization today eliminate all the bacteria? Hehe. It’s impossible. The Pangu civilization has gone extinct. Maybe the civilization of mankind will go extinct one day. But as long as the universe exists, germs will never

go extinct, will they?

“Therefore, even if we are really ants, dust, and insignificant bacteria and viruses, so what? It does not mean that our glorious civilization cannot have a place in the vast universe!

The bold and powerful declaration made Lu Qingchen’s soul shine stubbornly for the third time.

The dark barrier that sealed him was torn apart and burnt like a paper shell. Coerced by his scorching aura, it ran back to Fuxis like a soldier who had abandoned his armor.

“”Wow—”

The mental devil couldn’t help but remark, “Lu Qingchen is indeed marvelous. Even I can’t help but feel my blood boiling when I hear it. It’s true that everybody has been wandering in the sea of stars for a long time. There is no limit to the amount of time and space. Every mountain is taller than the last.

‘We will certainly meet countless civilizations that are stronger than us. How can we survive without the fearlessness when our heads are as big as bowls?

“Every inch has its advantages and every ruler has its shortcomings. The strength of a civilization cannot be generalized. Who says that dinosaurs are necessarily stronger than ants or bacteria?

“In the old days, the Blood Stripe Virus was not the most primitive bacteria, cells, spores, or viruses. It could still cruise in the universe on meteorites without being discovered by the tides. It was evident that the tides had limitations or loopholes, too. Otherwise, it would not have needed to rear such a

legion!

“That’s right. Although Lu Qingchen took the wrong path in the future, the determination deep down in his bones makes him a man of the federation.

Li Yao was deeply moved, too.. “It was not a waste of my enlightenment when he was little!”

Chapter 2854: No Right To Fear!

“If you keep acting like this…”

The mental devil said gloomily, “I have to consider moving closer to Lu Qingchen and standing on his side. After all, the young man seems to be a good guy. He is both bad and unyielding, unlike some people who are both good and shameless!

“No, no, no. I was just joking to ease the tension!”

Li Yao hurried to say, “I was greatly shocked when I heard the earthshaking truth about the ‘black curtain maker’. We can’t vent our anger by crying like ghosts and howling like wolves like Lu Qingchen. If we don’t relax for a moment, our souls are going to collapse. It’s really killing us!”

The mental devil sniffed and did not comment. It was too lazy to bother with Li Yao and continued eavesdropping on the conversation between Lu Qingchen and Fuxi.

“You are indeed… interesting.”

Faced with Lu Qingchen’s stubbornness, Fuxi was silent for a moment before he replied, “Sometimes, I really don’t understand. How can a human being’s feeble body and tiny soul boast such ambitions, such intense curiosity, and such suicidal arrogance?

“Maybe, it is the greatest advantage of mankind that we are different from the Pangu civilization and the prehistoric civilization!

Lu Qingchen grinned. “It is also the biggest reason why we are going further than you and the thousands of civilizations that used to live in the universe!

“Maybe we are ignorant, arrogant, and suicidal, but the prehistoric civilizations that seemed to be rational and wise and calculated in every way did not seem to be immortal! It is better to be suicidal than to wait to be killed.

“Excellent. If we can hear your words today, the experts and wise men of the Pangu civilization who were determined to create mankind against all odds will be able to rest in peace, won’t they?

“As I said just now, a newborn calf is not scared of a tiger,” Fuxi said solemnly. “Maybe, if a civilization wants to march out of the mother universe and go further, it needs a young, vigorous, and reckless spirit such as yours. When the Pangu civilization was preparing to march out of the mother universe,

they were too mature to the point that they were too timid and burdened by all kinds of burdens.

“At that time, the surviving soldiers of the expedition army, who were also the best super elites of the thirteen species of the Pangu Civilization, did not have the fearlessness and ignorance that you did. Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu! Hiu!

10,000 extremely painful memories piled up like a hill in his consciousness, almost crushing his soul.

suddenly, she became a mother who killed all her children because of negligence.

Bitterness. The sea of desire. Alll living creatures were suffering. Countless memory pieces were interconnecting in his soul and locking his brain like burning chains.

“The computational ability of the Fuxis is too high. Everything is under their control. We are walking right into their trap.
