It chased after the defeated soldiers and lunged at the atmosphere of the Jade Cauldron Planet, trying to further disrupt the communication and defense network that the enemy had set up on the planet

so that the subsequent troops could launch a strike in low-Earth orbit.

Boss Bai seized the opportunity to gather his troops and form an unstoppable scalpel again, ready to cut the cake.

But right then, Lei Chenghu’s command came over from ‘Thunder God’, the brand-new flagship of the coalition army.

“What? Stop attacking?”

Boss Bai was dazed for a moment. His face was immediately filled with fury.

Very soon, the highest-level secret communication channel between ‘Alloy Virus’ and ‘Thunder God’ was established. The two best commanders of the fleet in the sea of stars glared at each other.

“What is the meaning of this? Why did they stop attacking?”

Boss Bai slapped the control panel and said furiously, “General Hu, can’t you see that we are in a good situation right now? The defenders have completely collapsed. There is no way that we can stop the effective defense and counterattack in a short period of time. Also, with so many remnants of the

space fleet fleeing into the atmosphere, the command of the defenders on the ground will be further disrupted. This is our best opportunity!

“As long as we are fast enough and occupy the command center on the ground of Jade Cauldron Planet within three days, the entire planet will be within our reach!

“General Hu, do you not know the importance of Jade Cauldron Planet?

“This is the Song family, no, the largest arsenal, magical equipment, and crystal warehouse of the four electors’ families. God knows how many abundant resources are buried below the ground. A lot of weapons and supplies are piled up in the warehouse, too. For us, who are all alone and short of

logistics, it is a great help!

“Seeing that our army has cleared all the enemies in space and that a lot of resources are right in front of us, and that all the warriors who have been fighting bloody battles are waiting to resupply and rest on Jade Tripod, wouldn’t we be letting the tiger return to the mountain if we stop attacking now?

‘When the defenders come back to themselves and assume a stance of defense on Jade Tripod, how are we going to deal with them?

“Commander Bai, this is the reason why I have to stop the attack, because we cannot take down such a tough guy!

On the other side of the light beam, ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu’s face was black. There was no telling whether it was because he was worried or because of the smoke of war. He said, “As you said, there are four arsenals and magical equipment warehouses that are the largest in the families of the four

electors. There are air defense bases on the ground that look like iron forests, underground fortresses that are as complicated as a maze, canned food that can’t be finished in decades, and endless weapons and ammunition!

“Our biggest disadvantage right now is our lack of soldiers and time.

“Ma fierce battle, it is one thing to destroy the enemy’s fleet, but it is another thing to deploy our meager troops to occupy the countless fortresses on and below the planet.

“There are too many cases where the ancient army attacked a solid city and failed to conquer it even after twenty years of siege, not to mention that we are faced with a planet, a planet that the enemy has been preparing for hundreds of years!

“Te told you since the very beginning that the priority of our battle is to take down the enemy’s forces and cut off their legs!

“Right now, we have destroyed the two deep-space fleets deployed by the enemy in the Jade Tripod Sector. They are one of the few fleets in the Song family that boast the ability to perform large-scale space jumps. They have essentially cut off one of the enemy’s legs and significantly undermined the

enemy’s mobility. We have achieved the purpose of the battle. If we are too greedy for the resources of the Jade Tripod Sector and sink into the quagmire of ground combat, how long will it take for us to occupy the entire planet? One month? Three months? A year and a half? How many elites and soldiers

will be sacrificed during the occupation? The assault that was supposed to be unstoppable will be reduced to a sloppy security battle?

“Therefore, as I said before, don’t care about the gains and losses of a city, a planet, or even a Sector. The most important thing about time is time! Only by crushing the enemy’s forces in the shortest amount of time and raising the most intense fear in the territory of the four families can we crush the

damn rebels in one fell swoop!

“Stop attacking the surface of the planet and focus your attack on the orbital bombardment. Blow up the main factories, weapon factories, crystal suit centers, and mining centers of the enemy so that, for at least three months to half a year, the enemy will not be able to produce another starship from Jade

Cauldron. No matter how many enemies are hiding on the planet, they will not be able to fly out or perform a space jump. Our purpose will be achieved.

“Then, we will pretend that our army is attacking the Jade Tripod Planet on a large scale and lure the remaining mobile forces of the Song family and even the four major families to reinforce the planet. Then, we will surround and attack the reinforcements two to three times to further weaken the

enemy’s mobile forces so that they will not dare to show up easily. After that, we will launch a thunderous attack and charge at—this place!”

Lei Chenghu clenched his iron-hammer-like fists and smashed them into the light beam, smashing the brilliant stars into smithereens.

It was ‘Yellow Dragon Sector’, the capital city of the Song family.

“This is our only chance of victory.

Lei Chenghu’s eyes bulged, and he declared, “Straight ahead!”

Boss Bais face was as gloomy as before. His sweat had almost turned into solid magma. He was not convinced by Lei Chenghu at all. “General Hu’s plan is good, but you must consider the current situation of our army. Our army is made of several different fleets and has been engaged in multiple fierce

battles. The battle right now has cost us a lot of soldiers. We are in dire need of supplies and rest.

“Right now, the resource-rich supply point is right in front of us, but we can’t do anything about it. We have to grit our teeth and attack the reinforcements!

“Has it ever occurred to you, General Hu, that the reinforcements to the Jade Tripod Sector at this moment are definitely the last elites of the four major families? As the saying goes, a cornered animal will do whatever it wants. When the elites go mad, it will not be easy to defeat them.

“According to General Hu, our army will march into the capital city of the Song family in less than half a day after we defeat the elites? How demanding are the ammunition, fuel, management, and leadership, as well as the fighting will of the soldiers at the grassroots level? It is like walking through a

spider’s web in the sky of the deepest level of hell. One moment of carelessness, and they will be doomed forever. Do you know the craziness and difficulty of the serial plan?”

“Tknow.”

Lei Chenghu said solemnly, “It is exactly because I’m very clear about the difficulty of the strategy that I’m certain that the enemy will never expect that we are bold enough to attack them directly!

“After the consecutive defeats in the ‘Battle of the Seven Seas’ and the ‘Battle of the Capital’, the four families have been disloyal and on the verge of collapse.

“At that time, a symbolic event would be enough to push the seemingly magnificent fortresses to collapse into debris.

“The news that the Song family, as the leader of the four major families, has conquered the capital city of the Song family is a signature event’. Once the news spreads, the army of the four major families will no longer have any will to fight, and the citizens and the low-level Immortal Cultivators in their

territory will stand up and resist, too. The governing order of the four major families will collapse like an avalanche. By then, even the impregnable fortresses such as the Jade Cauldron Planet will surrender without a fight!

Chapter 2874: The Federation’S Choice

“As expected of General Hu. What an impressive strategy.”

Boss Bai did not give in at all. He said coldly, “But no matter how big the pie is, it will only count if it can be eaten. Have you ever thought about what to do if the combat ability of our army is not enough to support continuous battles and long-distance assaults? The so-called ‘straight to the enemy’s base’,

in other words, is a long expedition with a lone army. By then, as long as the tiniest accident occurs, the entire army might be wiped out!

“The safest strategy is to take over Jade Tripod and transform the resource-rich planet into our forward base and the pivot of our logistics supply line.

“Safety? There’s no time for that!”

Lei Chenghu’s face was even darker than before. He said solemnly, “The movements of the fleet of the Covenant Alliance in the battlefield in the west are getting more and more ambiguous. They might launch a fatal attack toward the center of the imperium at any moment. If I were the commander of the

main fleet of the Covenant Alliance, it is very likely that I would have stabbed my sharpest blade into the heart of the imperium!

“When the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation shows up in the capital city, it will be even more impossible for the remaining members of the four electors’ families to surrender. We will be caught between a rock and a hard place. By then, what can we do even if we occupy a tiny planet?

“That is not a good reason to attack the enemy’s capital city when we are exhausted and running out of supplies!

Boss Bais fury burst out again. He bellowed, “How are we going to fight a war if we don’t rest?”

“Even if we can’t hold on, we have to hold on. Right now, we are fighting with our last breath and winning one victory after another to stimulate the troops to charge at the enemy in one go. If we stop and rest, our breath will disperse, and our combat ability will hit rock bottom. We will never be able to

stand up again!”

Lei Chenghu declared, “This is a battle that risks the fate of the imperium. Even though we know that the odds are slim, we have no other options!

“No. You don’t have a choice, not ‘we.

Boss Bai narrowed his eyes and said coldly, “Whatever General Hu says, I still think that your strategy is too risky. My subordinates have no reason to do such a thing for the imperium. We have our own missions and missions.

The two of them fell silent at the same time.

It was true that the two of them had been at loggerheads since the very beginning.

With Lei Chenghu’s expertise in commanding, how could he not know how reckless and risky it would be if he did not resupply and reorganize his troops, which had been pushed to their limits?

With Boss Bai’s unpredictable hunting and looting, how could he not know the importance of ‘time’ and ‘mobility’? How could he not know how troublesome and idiotic it was to pry open the crust of a planet with a fleet?

Thinking back to his life as a space pirate, he had been famous for running away after a battle. Was there something wrong with his head? Why did he want to attack a planet where so many soldiers were gathered? Why did he want to fight a goddamn ground battle? Why did he want to fight a civil war?

However, the stance of the two of them and their mission forced them to ignore the risks and loopholes in their plan. They could only hope that a ‘miracle’ would occur so that they could finish the job in one shot.

Lei Chenghu had to take care of the surviving members of the four electors’ families before the main fleet of the Covenant Alliance showed up. Every fleet that could perform a space jump would seal the remaining members of the four families on every lone planet.

Boss Bai, on the other hand, was trying to preserve his strength in order to win more chips for the game between the federation and the imperium.

After all, Boss Bai did not care about the difference between Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators. As a space pirate, he was not a saint.

However, it was one thing to help the Immortal Cultivators fight a war, and it was another thing to help the Immortal Cultivators collect the chestnuts from the fire at the risk of death.

Such silence also revealed the biggest weakness of the rebelling coalition army, which was the lack of unity of command.

Lei Chenghu was the nominal commander-in-chief of the rebel army. Boss Bai and the fleet of arsonists were all under his control.

However, there was no way that Boss Bai would really hand over the military power to someone else. Of course, it was a self-sustaining system that could only work together.

The continuous victories were still acceptable, but when they were faced with a tough battle in which the entire team was likely to be wiped out, the cracks between the two of them were beyond repair.

Faced with the unruly Boss Bai, Lei Chenghu did not have a good solution, either.

He wouldn’t tum against his only strong ally at such a critical moment, would he?

“Any news about the reinforcements of the federation?”

He could only change the topic to ease the tension.

It was true that, if the reinforcements of the Star Glory Federation arrived in time, the number of soldiers who could resist the coalition army would increase significantly, and the strategists would be much more at ease. With the capability of Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai, they would certainly be able to

swallow the remaining members of the four major families alive.

“Not yet. It is still unclear whether or not the federation is willing to send reinforcements and how many reinforcements they can send.”

Boss Bai said gloomily, “It was a good lesson for you, General Hu. A few years ago, the war machine of the Star Glory Federation was still functioning to defend against the Imperium of True Human Beings. It even fought a solid war against the Black Wind Fleet that belonged to the imperium.

“Although the Black Wind Fleet did not invade the heartland of the federation and commit crimes such as robbery, countless children of the federation still sacrificed their precious lives in the battle.

“The hostility and wariness of the federation toward the imperium have seeped into the blood and bones of every citizen of the federation during the hundred years of education. Right now, how difficult do you think it is to make every citizen of the federation make a 180-degree turn within a few months

so that the federal army, which we have painstakingly trained for a hundred years, will fight to ‘defend the imperium?

“It’s true that Li Yao is trapped in the depths of the sea of stars, and his wife is the Speaker of Parliament. But the Speaker of Parliament is not the emperor of the imperium. The decision on whether to send troops or to declare war on the Holy Accord Alliance is in the hands of the parliament. The

decisions of the parliament must reflect the will of all the citizens. If all the citizens are against sending troops both in society and in the circle of public opinion, the parliament and the Speaker of Parliament will not be able to defy the universal order.

“Therefore, this is why the imperium does not need the damn parliament.

Lei Chenghu gnashed his teeth. “The idiotic parliament, the idiotic citizens, and the idiotic so-called ‘public opinion’ are merely the ideas of a bunch of pigs to bind a ferocious tiger. They will only drag the Star Glory Federation into a dead end. Don’t you understand the simple logic of ‘when the lips are

gone, the teeth will be cold? If the imperium is destroyed and the Covenant Alliance rises, will the federation be able to hide in a corner of the sea of stars and live a peaceful life? It’s just a matter of time!”

“Lunderstand. I think so, too. But it’s too late now. We can only wait for the federation to make a decision.

Boss Bai looked at Lei Chenghu and finally made a concession. “General Hu, even if you don’t agree to attack Jade Tripod, can you at least ask your troops to take down ‘Jade Child’, the main satellite of Jade Tripod? The docks, crystal mines, refining centers, and weapon factories on it will be of great help

to our army, which is short of resources.

“Anyways, if you are going to play the trick of ‘surround the enemy and attack their reinforcements’, you have to make sure that the enemy is fooled, don’t you?

Lei Chenghu stared at Boss Bai for a long time.

“Alright. Three days.”

His neck was like a rusted gear. He nodded hard and extended three mechanical fingers. “In three days, we must breach the defense line of ‘Jade Star’ and take over the satellite. Let’s hope that good news comes from the Star Glory Federation before that!”

The light beam gradually dimmed.

Lei Chenghu’s face was replaced by a dark vacuum where stars were burning and planets were glittering like cakes.

Watching everything, Boss Bai’s hollow eyes seemed to have pierced through the three-dimensional universe and reached somewhere far away.

After a long time, he sighed and expressed the same feelings as Lu Qingchen did.

“Federation, my Federation!”

At the same time, at the edge of the sea of stars, in the Heavenly Ring Sector of the Star Glory Federation.

While Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai were having a heated debate about the strategy to march into the space port, on the super orbital dock at the periphery of Heaven’s Ring, a large mothership that had just been modified was slowly sailing away from the space port, towed by the towing starship. It could not

wait to join a brand-new enormous fleet that was as brilliant as the stars before it was tested.

On the mothership, an intense argument broke out in the secret meeting room.

The parties to the dispute included the leadership of the federal government, the experts of various forces, and the generals and experts of the military and the academic world.

Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliament of the federation, Jin Xinyue, the previous president of the Dark Moon Foundation, Bai Kaixin, the chief of staff of the federal army, Peng Hai, Meng Chixin, Han Baling… The resounding names and the faces that appeared on the headlines every day were

indicative of the importance of the meeting.

‘What they were concerned about was naturally the news between Li Yao and the imperium. They were also arguing about whether or not the federation would send troops and how far they would be involved in the swirl at the center of the sea of stars.

Such a meeting should’ve been held in the capital city of the federation.

However, it was too sensitive whether or not the federation was going to interfere with the situation of the imperium, especially the latest news that Li Yao had gone missing. It was too serious and destructive. If it was exposed by the media, who knew what kind of earthshaking waves it would set off in

the entire federation? Even the morale of the army would be halved before they set off.

Therefore, the leadership of the federation decided to keep a low profile and conduct preliminary discussions and deductions in the forum of the think tanks. When they came up with a general solution, they would release all the news, especially the disappearance of Li Yao, to the entire federation.

Chapter 2875: Kunlun Fleet

Therefore, the experts from the nine worlds of the Star Glory Federation did not gather in the capital city, the Heaven’s Origin Sector, which was the most important industrial world far away from the capital city.

Even Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliament, had come to this place in the name of ‘inspecting the troops’.

Of course, the fleet that was being refined, modified, and upgraded in the orbital docks of the Heaven’s Ring Sector and the other space rings deserved a thorough inspection.

It was the third deep-space fleet that boasted super long-range space jumps after the Burning Prairie Fleet and the Great White Fleet.

The core of the warship was Nuwa’s warship, which had been excavated from the depths of the icy plain of the Ancient Sacred World.

The warship, as well as the Pangu Underground Palace buried deep below the warship, stored countless secret treasures and secrets that corresponded to the technology of the Kunlun Mystic Realm that the federation had been studying for a hundred years. The technology reserves and cutting-edge

exploration abilities of the federation had advanced by leaps and bounds.

The federation had employed a lot of Kunlun technology to repair and modify Nuwa’s warship. They had even dismantled a lot of magical equipment units from the Kunlun Mysterious Realm and installed them on the warship. Finally, the warship regained the ability to cruise in the sea of stars in the

shortest amount of time. By conservative estimation, 30% of its previous combat ability had been restored.

30% of the combat ability of the primeval era was a shocking figure, too. It was much higher than Prairie Fire, the previous flagship of the federal army.

Therefore, after the Nuwa warship was named ‘Kunlun’, it did not join the Burning Prairie Fleet directly but became the core of the new fleet.

According to the tradition of the federal army, the flagship was named ‘Kunlun’. Naturally, the entire fleet was named ‘Kunlun Fleet’.

It was a fleet with a strong smell of experiment and exploration. It was also a new fleet that boasted a lot of technologies. The primeval technology from the Ancient Sacred World and the Kunlun Mystic Realm, the imperial technology from the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet, and the original

technology of the federation and the biochemical technology of the Blood Demon Sector had all been consolidated in the new fleet, making it look completely different from the Burning Prairie Fleet, the Black Wind Fleet, and any other fleet of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance. It

was a super fleet that was truly oriented to the future and could leap into infinity.

‘The Burning Prairie Fleet was the core of the federal army. A slight change could affect the entire fleet. Since they had just fought a bloody battle against the Black Wind Fleet of the Star Ocean Imperium a few years ago, the officers and soldiers of the fleet still had a deep hatred for the Immortal

Cultivators. It was impossible for them to express their stance on whether or not to support the Star Ocean Imperium.

The origins of the Kunlun Fleet were complicated. The technology was advanced, the personnel were integrated, and there were not many historical baggage. Therefore, the Kunlun Fleet was the most suitable choice for an expedition to the center of the sea of stars.

But there were still many contradictions that needed to be resolved.

At the very least, the chief of staff of the federal army could represent the military. He had a different opinion when he gazed at Bai Kaixin in the depths of the sea of stars with a frown.

“The military is not against the expedition to the center of the sea of stars. Although we paid a high price in the bloody battle against the imperial army a few years ago and are still in the recovery phase, we know better than anybody that hatred is not enough to blind us.

Bai Kaixin raised his eyebrow and looked around the meeting table. He glanced at Ding Lingdang, Guo Chunfeng, Jin Xinyue, Meng Chixin, Han Baling, Di Feiwen, and so on. He paused for a moment and continued. “However, I hope that you can consider carefully whether or not it is the best time to send

your troops.

“First question. It’s been less than five years since the ‘Federation Defense War’. The federation suffered tremendous losses in the war. Countless elite troops haven’t recovered to this day. The people are not very enthusiastic about the war, either. There is a saying that goes ‘if the federation is saved, the

war will be over forever’.

“At that time, countless children of the federation will have to go to billions of lightyears away to bleed and sacrifice themselves. The public opinion and the morale of the troops, including the economic model in peacetime, will all be seriously affected.

“The federation is like a balloon that has been under the pressure of the Black Wind Fleet for the past hundred years. The balloon has been expanding, and every inch of the surface of the balloon has been tightened. But during the ‘Federation Defense War’ a few years ago, all the pressure was released, and

the balloon suddenly deflated.

“Now, we are going to ask the balloon to expand to its limits again in only a few months. We cannot do that no matter what!

“Second question. I believe that, after you’ve watched the modification, refinement, and combat drills of the main force warships of the ‘Kunlun Fleet’, you’ve seen that the newest, sharpest deep-space fleet of the federation is far from the final integration and upgrade. The infinite potential of the

technology of the imperium, the federal technology, and the primeval technology has not been fully exploited yet! If the fleet is given another ten years, no, five years, no, even three years, its combat ability will be doubled, tripled, and even more!

“The time we have is too short. The time we have to build up and get to know each other is too short. If we go into battle in such a hurry, it will be tantamount to turning the future general into a child soldier. It will be a great waste and a crime. The endless potential of the Kunlun Fleet will be sacrificed in

vain!

“Therefore, we are not against the expedition to the center of the sea of stars. But if we postpone it for another two years and let the propaganda machine of the federation be fully activated, we will be able to explain the significance of the expedition to the civilians and the soldiers at the grassroots level.

We will also be able to fully digest the technology of the Star Ocean Imperium and bring out the best combat ability of the Kunlun Fleet. At that time, if we reinforce the Star Ocean Imperium, will we be more confident and have less negative influence on the federation?

“Everybody here is an experienced soldier. Although speed is of paramount importance for a soldier, it is not unusual for a battle to last three to five years in the boundless sea of stars. Take the ‘war of the federation’ for example. Although the outcome of the war was determined within several days, it still

took almost a year for the war to come to an end, didn’t it? And this is the result of… General Di’s understanding of the situation and the ‘rebel on the battlefield’.

“The Star Glory Federation and the Star Glory Federation each boast hundreds of Sectors, countless resource planets, and star fortresses. It is perfectly normal that the strategical war will last five years or even longer.

“If so, wouldn’t it be better if we could focus our attention on the development of our army, accumulate our strength, and wait for the Holy Accord Alliance’s edge to be worn down before we suddenly attack and cut its throat?”

Chief of Staff Bai’s words were neither sincere nor unfair.

But the ruthlessness that was hidden behind the sincerity and fairness was clear to everyone present.

He could only say that he was definitely going to send his troops. With such a heated battle in the center of the sea of stars, how could the federation not send their troops to fish in troubled waters or rob someone else?

But if they were really going to send out their troops, it was not the time to do so yet. They should let the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance fight first so that they could tear each other apart and bleed to the last drop of their blood. When both sides were down to their last drop of blood, the

federal army could clean up the mess. Wouldn’t it be much better than joining the battle right now?

It couldn’t be helped. Although Bai Kaixin was a real Cultivator, he was too merciful to command an army. The competition between two countries could be as cruel as that.

After all, his every bit of ‘mercy’ might cost the precious lives of countless children of the federation.

After Bai Kaixin finished his speech, many experts had different expressions on their faces.

Most of the experts from the ‘Old Federation’ agreed with him. They valued their chips and were not willing to take risks easily.

The experts from the new worlds such as the ‘Ancient Sacred World’ were much calmer. The development of the new worlds was not as fast as that of the Core Worlds. The experts from the new worlds such as the Ancient Saints, the Heaven’s Ring, and the Crystal Sector were often slower in the federal

system, too.

They were not against a large-scale expedition that would benefit the federation and break the old structure so that they could rise up in the future.

Especially the ‘Twelve Ancient Saints’.

Led by ‘Meng Chixin’ and ‘Wu Suiyun’, the spectral Cultivators in the Divinity Transformation Stage, the twelve experts were in an awkward position in the system of the Star Glory Federation.

In terms of personal capability, the twelve of them combined were definitely one of the strongest groups in the federation, especially the two spectral Cultivators and the unreasonable ‘Sword Addict’ Yan Liren.

However, in terms of comprehensive capability, the ‘Special Administrative Region of Ancient Saints’ was still feudal. Their technology and social development were thousands of years behind other Sectors. Even the ‘Crystal Sector’, the weakest world in the desert, was far behind.

In terms of resources, there were well-preserved prehistoric starships and all kinds of ancient treasures in the Ancient Sacred World. The melee combat ability of the ancient Cultivators was much higher than that of other worlds, too. The ‘Kunlun’ below their feet had been excavated from the Ancient

Sacred World, too.

But in regard to technology, they did not have the ability to repair and utilize the primeval technology, the ancient treasures, and the giant soldier gods perfectly. Without the technical support of the federation, they could only watch the countless ancient treasures embedded deep inside the crust.

It was even possible that countless civilians would lose their homes, starve to death, and be trapped in the cycle of life and death for tens of thousands of years.

Although the federation offered preferential treatment and all kinds of support to the Ancient Saint World, the unparalleled experts such as Meng Chixin, Han Baling, and Yan Liren were not the kind of people who would beg for mercy or receive charity.

Naturally, they hoped that they and their hometown could make greater contributions to the federation and expand their voice so that they could be part of the federation.

Wasn’t a magnificent war that allowed the Star Glory Federation to truly stand at the peak of the sea of stars the perfect opportunity for them to prove themselves and for the rapid development of the Ancient Saint World?

Chapter 2876: Selling Umbrella On A Sunny Day

However, although the ancient experts thought that way, they did not have enough theoretical knowledge to express their opinions and interests clearly. After all, they had jumped from the ‘feudal era’ to an information society with abundant productivity. A few years ago, they had been fighting hard for

the feudal sovereignty on a continent. It would be too much if they were asked to make decisions on the unpredictable dominion of the sea of stars.

They, as well as the experts of the ‘New Four Sectors’ who had agreed to send troops, all looked at the same person—Di Feiwen, who used to be the deputy commander of the Black Wind Fleet and was now the president of the ‘Boneyard—Ultimacy Development Committee.

Of all the experts present, only one of them was sincere enough to help the imperium at any cost. It was undoubtedly the Immortal Cultivator of the former imperium, Di Feiwen.

No. The word ‘original’ was redundant. To this moment, Di Feiwen had not abandoned his identity as a citizen of the imperium and an Immortal Cultivator. He still kept the ambiguous ‘dual nationality’.

Di Feiwen’s experience and his position today reflected the unpredictable ups and downs of fate.

A hundred years ago, he was just an ordinary deacon of the Infinite Commerce Alliance. At that time, the Infinite Commerce Alliance was just an ordinary business alliance in a remote world of the imperium. It was just a loose association where countless small merchants huddled together for warmth,

shivering in the clutches of the imperial power and the nobles.

He had merely relied on his bloodline from the Black Wind Sector to first become the liaison between the Black Wind Sector and the Infinite Commerce Alliance. Then, he gradually became the manager of the Infinite Commerce Alliance’s resource delivery to the Black Wind Fleet. In the end, he turned into

the vice commander of the Black Wind Fleet.

However, it was not the right time for him to be the vice commander-in-chief. It was the moment when the defense line of the Black Wind Sector was riddled with holes, when the starships of the Black Wind Sector were fleeing in panic, and when the situation was desperate.

If that were not the case, he would not have been appointed as the deputy commander-in-chief despite his background as a merchant and a logistic officer who knew nothing about fleet command and the sea of stars.

When the Black Wind Fleet rushed to the edge of the sea of stars with tremendous resources, nobody was optimistic about the future of the fleet, much less Di Feiwen himself.

The untamable ‘Black Wind King’ was not willing to be manipulated by anybody. Even the Divine Martial Emperor at that time could not command him, not to mention the Infinite Commerce Alliance and Di Feiwen.

At best, Di Feiwen was the master of Night Brightness and the Dark Night Family. A loyal God of Wealth, including Di Feiwen himself, thought so, too.

All he needed to do was to manage the resources of the entire fleet. As for the specific strategies and tactics, he had no say in them at all. Of course, he did not have too many private soldiers to command more power.

‘When the Black Wind Fleet was almost annihilated in front of the Star Glory Federation, Di Feiwen’s fate hit rock bottom. He was about to die a graveless death among the dark stars.

Right then, fate played an absurd joke on him.

Dark Night Brightness—including all the most fanatical militarists and warmongers in the Black Wind Fleet—was dead. The remaining survivors had scattered in the sea of stars and lost all their will to fight. They were all at a loss and wouldn’t let go of their last hope. Even Di Feiwen, the God of Wealth

who never had much prestige, could recruit and command them easily.

The Star Glory Federation did not have such a big appetite, either. After devouring the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet, they had no choice but to grant him the right to ‘rebellion on the battlefield’. He was welcomed with a decent peace and a planet with abundant resources, which used to be Boneyard.

But now, Ultimacy had become their base for rest and recovery.

This was the beginning of Di Feiwen’s good luck, but it was far from the end of his meteoric rise.

According to common logic, Di Feiwen’s actions were a complete betrayal of the imperium. Although he, as the ‘defeated general’, had been able to lead the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet and live in the federation for a long time, gradually integrating into the federation and becoming a high-ranking

general in the federation, he was an unforgivable traitor in the eyes of the Imperium of True Human Beings. He was going to be pinned to the pillar of shame, skinned, and skinned.

But that was the logic of the four electors’ families.

The ‘New Empire’ governed by the revolutionaries apparently had different views.

In order to consolidate their power, the revolutionaries had to find powerful allies in the sea of stars. They were even willing to cooperate with authentic Cultivators. They could even turn a blind eye to the identity and belief of ‘Vulture Li Yao’ and grant him the title of ‘Black Wind King’. Why would they

care about the past of Di Feiwen and the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet?

In fact, as the sole intermediary between the Star Ocean Imperium and the federation, Di Feiwen and the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet were getting more and more important day by day, and their status was soaring, too.

The officials of the new empire and the new federation both ignored the awkward identities of Di Feiwen and the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet. The new emperor, the young lion who was in desperate need of external help when he had just ascended the throne, even granted the title of ‘Second Class

Marquis’ to the former peddler as a reward for his ‘loyalty’ and ‘mutual bravery’.

A second-grade marquis. A second-grade marquis of the Imperium of True Human Beings!

A hundred years ago, countless experts from the noble families of the four electors would not have been granted the title of ‘Second Class Marquis’ even if they had spent their entire lives fighting against each other.

Even during the counterattack of the Star Ocean Imperium, during the special period when the titles were distributed in batches like hats, to wear the Medal of the Sun and the Moon, which represented a second-grade marquis, on one’s chest, one would have to make real contributions and be forged with

tons of iron and blood!

If all the second-rank nobles of the Star Ocean Imperium learned that Di Feiwen had been living a happy life on Boneyard with the remnants of the Black Wind Fleet, they would definitely crawl out of their coffins in fury. Even if they were burnt to ashes, their ashes would be condensed again.

However, since he knew that his identity was quite complicated and that his stance was quite awkward, Di Feiwen kept a low profile after he was granted the title of second marquis of the Star Ocean Imperium. He also showed more ‘loyalty’ to the Star Ocean Imperium. In the remote negotiations between

the Star Ocean Imperium and the Star Ocean Imperium, he stood firmly on the Star Ocean Imperium’s side and slashed the negotiators of the Star Ocean Imperium multiple times, helping the Star Ocean Imperium exploit a lot of precious technologies.

Di Feiwen was very clear that the life and wealth of the Black Wind Legion and himself depended on the federation, not the imperium.

The stronger the federation was, the more important they were to the imperium, and the more valuable they were.

If they helped the imperium to deal with the federation, or even annihilate the federation, it would be the time when the imperium would investigate the crimes of the ‘shameless rebels’. All the nobles would be beheaded.

Di Feiwen had made his stance very clear. A sensitive topic such as an expedition to the center of the sea of stars was not something that a general of two identities such as himself could interfere with easily.

Therefore, at the beginning of the meeting, Di Feiwen had been sitting straight with his eyes and nose lowered, as if he were an old monk meditating. His mouth seemed to be zipped, and he was determined to keep silent until the end.

He even ignored Han Baling’s undisguised gaze.

Speaking of which, Di Feiwen was quite close to the ancient experts, especially ‘Han Baling’, the king of the Ghost Qin Grassland.

Most of the ancient experts were not pure Cultivators, especially the ambitious ‘classical militarists’ such as Han Baling. They were more interested in the Great Dao of Cultivation in the imperium than in the seemingly gentle system of Cultivators in the federation.

In order to cure the famine, plague, and other diseases caused by the population explosion of the Ancient Saint World, as well as to develop Boneyard on a large scale, the federation had shipped a lot of immigrants from the Ancient Saint World to Boneyard to live. As a result, Di Feiwen and Han Ba Ling

had a lot of work opportunities.

Han Baoling was different from Yan Liren and Master Bitter Cicada. He was very ambitious in politics and was keenly aware of the ambiguous relationship between the federation and the imperium. He also knew the reason for Di Feiwen’s meteoric rise. Also, it was true that the two of them were on par

with each other. After only several years, they had formed a close, political alliance.

Right now, the ancient Saints craved for a glorious war to improve their position in the federation, but they did not have a clear reason to refute Bai Kaixin’s questions. Therefore, they pinned their hopes on Di Feiwen.

It was possible that Di Feiwen was trying to make friends with the giants of the new worlds such as the Ancient Sacred World in order to keep the ‘old federal system’ in check so that the remnants of the Black Wind Legion would have a stronger independence and a brighter future.

But right now, with everybody watching, it was even more inappropriate for him to reveal his thoughts.

It was not until Ding Lingdang, the Speaker of Parliaments, looked at him with her sharp eyes that a bitter smile appeared on his face.

“General Di, the imperium is your hometown. If the federation really sends troops to the center of the sea of stars, you will have to play an important role, including the cooperation of the federal army and the imperial army, the safety of the federal army, and… the negotiation of the treaty and the

conditions after the victory. You will be of great help.

Ding Lingdang asked straightforwardly, “What do you think about the concerns that Chief of Staff Bai raised just now?”

This was forcing him to tear off the pretense of uncertainty and ambiguity.

Di Feiwen sighed in his heart. For some reason, he recalled his life in his hometown of the Star Ocean Imperium a hundred years ago.

It was not necessarily a warm, beautiful memory.

But no matter how dark and cold it was, it was still his hometown!

“It’s been a long time since I commanded a fleet. No. I should say that I’ve never commanded a fleet before. I’m probably the most incompetent ‘flotilla commander-in-chief in the universe, aren’t I? Naturally, Chief of Staff Bai has the final say in the military decisions.

Di Feiwen smiled and said, “From the bottom of my heart, I’ve always been a businessman. I’m just a petty merchant who buys low and sells high.

“As a businessman, I would like to ask you a question.. If a businessman wants to sell his umbrella at a high price, should he choose a sunny day or a rainy day?

Chapter 2877: Building The “Federalists” Within The Empire

While talking, Di Feiwen squinted at Han Baling.

Han Baling immediately took the hint and replied, “Of course, when the storm is coming!”

“Wrong.”

Di Feiwen smiled and said, “It’s true that umbrellas can’t be sold at a good price when the sky is clear, but what’s the point of buying umbrellas when it’s raining cats and dogs and all the passers-by are soaked?

“Therefore, an umbrella must be sold at a high price. The best time to sell it is not when it is sunny or when it is raining, but when a storm is brewing!

“In the war between the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance, although it was a protracted battle between hundreds of Sectors, thousands of planets, and millions of starships, the real ‘winners’ were undoubtedly the most critical battles, especially when we gathered intelligence from various

sources and predicted the coming ‘Battle of the Capital’.

“The mysterious main fleet of the Covenant Alliance is sharpening their blades in secret. When the rebels of the revolutionaries are deeply entangled with the remnants of the four electors’ families and cannot free themselves, the shining sabers of the Covenant Alliance will definitely break the feeble

defense line of the Empyrean Terminus Sector.

“Judging from the fact that the revolutionaries are in trouble both internally and externally, with barely enough soldiers and yet to be pacified, if the capital city is lost, the entire front line will collapse. By then, the defeated devil fire will trigger a chain reaction among hundreds of Sectors and lead to an

avalanche-like collapse. The people of the Covenant Alliance will take the opportunity to march forward unstoppably. Who can stop them? Let alone two to three to five years, I’m afraid that the people of the Covenant Alliance will be able to lock onto the victory within a year and a half.

“At that time, the imperium, which had been on the brink of destruction, was like a passer-by who had been soaked in a storm. However large and solid the federation was, how could it be of any use and sell at a high price?

“The other possibility is that, assuming that the main fleet of the Star Glory Federation is not as strong as we expected, or that the rebels of the revolutionaries miraculously crush all the remaining members of the four electors’ families and return to defend the Empyrean Terminus Sector in time, the

outcome of the war will be decided in the next two to three years.

“If the imperium could compete with the Covenant Alliance with its own strength, what reason would it have to spend so much money to win the federation over? What reason would a man who had just experienced a sunny day have to buy an umbrella?

“Allin all, if the federation does not march to the center of the sea of stars right now, there will be no need for it ever again, because the target you want to ‘rescue’ in two to three years will either be gone or defeated by the enemy alone. There will be no need for rescue at all.

“If that is the case, the federation should concentrate all the resources on building a magnificent fleet of immigrants and flee to the black domain at the end of the sea of stars with all the important figures and strategical resources, because the federation will not be let go of no matter who wins the war

between the imperium and the Covenant Alliance.

“General Di, watch your language.”

Bai Kaixin said coldly, “The federation does not accept threats in any way.

“Tm not threatening you. I’m just stating facts. In fact, if the army of the imperium does march toward the federation one day, it will not be you but the Immortal Cultivators who have surrendered who will suffer the most.

With a bitter smile, Di Feiwen said, “You are the ‘enemies’, and we are the ‘traitors’. Compared to the ‘enemies’, people hate traitors more. Enemies can be recruited and negotiated, but the traitors must be executed.

“Therefore, believe me, the Immortal Cultivators of the ‘Black Wind Sector’ cherish our life in the federation more than any other Cultivator does. We don’t want the imperium to extend its claws to the peaceful land at the edge of the sea of stars, nor do we want it to fall into the hands of the imperium

again.

“However, the development of the situation often does not change according to our will. If the federation turns a blind eye to the perilous situation in the center of the sea of stars and tries to take advantage of the situation, destruction will be our only outcome.

“Let me repeat myself. This is the best time to maximize the value of the technology. Opportunity cannot be missed. Yesterday, we received a package of the latest batch of technology from the Star Glory Federation, including ‘Liquefaction of Dragon Bud Crystals’, ‘Model Spiritual Shields in a 3,000-degree

high-temperature environment’, ‘Optimization Algorithm for Regular Cruises’, and other very important technologies. They were all offered to the federation for free, including the establishment of a special zone for Cultivators and a springboard for the advancement of the Star Glory Federation in the

center of the sea of stars under the name of the ‘Black Wind King Autonomous Territory’. It is not entirely unnegotiable.

“Think about it, everybody. If the revolutionaries had not been in desperate need of reinforcements such as the federation, would it have been possible to reach such a compromise beyond all the principles?

“We admit that the conditions of the new imperium seem very sincere, but as the saying goes, no matter how big the pie is, we have to be able to eat it. Even if we have to eat it, we have to be wary of the hooks inside the pie.

Bai Kaixin said, “It is a taboo to march alone. Compared with the imperium and the Covenant Alliance, the federation is too weak. Three deep-space fleets, namely the Burning Prairie Fleet, the Kunlun Fleet, and the Great White Fleet, will be at the mercy of the federation if one or two of them are killed in

the center of the sea of stars!

“There’s no need to underestimate yourself, Chief of Staff Bai. After the technological exchange over the past few years, I believe you all know that the military strength of the federation is not as weak as we imagined. It is even higher than that of the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance in

many fields.

“There are two reasons why the federation is in the lead,” Di Feiwen said unhurriedly. “The first is that the development of the Kunlun Mystic Realm has brought a lot of primeval technology, which has been significantly improved and strengthened by the relics below the Ancient Saint World.

“Secondly, the federation will excavate a lot of intact Giant God Soldiers and relatively intact components from the Kunlun Mystic Realm and the underground of the Ancient Saint World and build a legion of ‘Giant God Soldiers’, which will have an enormous number of soldiers per person. In a space

battleground, hundreds of Giant God Soldiers will be deployed at the same time!

“I believe that I don’t need to elaborate on how terrifying the enhancement of the Giant God Soldier is. Even if the top-tier combat ability of the Cultivators of the federation is not as good as that of the Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium, with the enhancement of the Giant God Soldier, it is

still possible to crush them.

“The weakness of the federation is obvious, too. Compared with the center of the sea of stars, the federation is short of resources and population. It is not suitable for prolonged battles such as war of attrition and war of scorched earth.

“No matter how elite the Kunlun Fleet is, no matter how many Giant God Soldiers there are, both the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance have ten times, or even a hundred times, of your strength. The federation won’t be able to withstand a five-to-one, or even ten-to-one, battlefield

exchange.

“General Han Baling, you are a seasoned warrior. In your opinion, should such a federal army fight a quick battle or be deployed to a seesaw battlefield?”

“A quick duel, of course!”

Han Baling replied without any hesitation, “The federal army today is like a sharp, unstoppable saber that can kill any enemy as long as they find a critical location!

“On the other hand, the thinner and faster the saber is, the easier it will be worn and broken, It will be very troublesome if the saber falls into the enemy’s scales!

“Yes. That’s why I suggested that the federation send out troops as soon as possible in time for the battle of the capital city.

Di Feiwen said, “Only in short, specific, and crucial battles such as the ‘Battle of the Capital’ can the federal army make best use of their advantages and seize the most brilliant victories, or even the final victory!

“On the other hand, in two to three years, the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance will have a protracted tug-of-war between hundreds of Sectors. It will be a competition of attrition and logistics. By then, two or three more fleets of the federation will be no more than two or three fewer

fleets. Do you want the best troops of the federation to be deployed into such a bloody, bottomless pit?

“That being said…”

Bai Kaixin pondered for a moment and said, “But who can guarantee that the Star Ocean Imperium will not harbor ill intentions when the best fleets of the federation, including most of the Giant God Soldiers, are deployed to the center of the sea of stars?

“You must know that, once we are involved in the swirl of a war, everything will be beyond our control. By then, our logistics will be hindered by the people of the Star Ocean Imperium. Who can guarantee that the Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium will cooperate wholeheartedly and hand

over the confidential technology and the prosperous world to the federation without backstabbing us?

“I can guarantee that.”

With a sincere face, Di Feiwen said, “The Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium are definitely not sincere about the cooperation. They are definitely scheming in their cruel and unscrupulous hearts. They will definitely be merciless and stab us in the back.”

“Well—”

Bai Kaixin and the other experts of the federation were all dumbfounded. They looked at Di Feiwen in a daze, not knowing what he meant.

“There are no eternal friends in the universe, only eternal interests. Of course, the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium are not cooperating with the Cultivators of the federation sincerely, but it does not stop them from forming an unbreakable ‘common interest group’ and guarantee the safety of the

expedition army of the federation with their interests.

Di Feiwen said confidently, “The imperium today is not a monolithic place. The selfish Immortal Cultivators do not have the will to fight against the Cultivators just for the ‘different paths’. The conflicts among the major forces of the imperium are too intense for the federation to ignore.

“For example, why does the federation have to help the revolutionaries eliminate the remaining members of the four families? Is it possible to keep the survivors of the four families as bargaining chips for negotiations with the revolutionaries?

“Another example. Inside the Reformists, the ‘Universal Commerce Alliance System’, led by Jin Yuyan, the ‘Deep Sea Fleet System’, led by Empress Dowager Li Linghai, and the ‘Military System’, led by Lei Chenghu, are actually divided. Will His Majesty Yon;

under the pressure of the three forces?

be willing to be the mascot and puppet forever

“Hehe. Although I haven’t had much contact with His Majesty, who is known as the ‘Golden Lion’, I have a vague feeling that His Majesty does not want to be manipulated by anybody, be it Lei Chenhu, Jin Yuyan, or his mother, Li Linghai.

“What is even better is that His Majesty does not seem to be interested in the fundamentalist path of immortality. It is very likely that he will go further than Li Linghai in the path of innovation.

“Then, is it possible for the federation to reach an agreement with Your Majesty in secret to make Your Majesty the most important member of the pro-federal faction, and then help Your Majesty suppress Lei Chenghu, Jin Yuyan, Li Linghai, and the other factions in secret to push forward Your Majesty’s

ideal revolution?”

“After all, Li Yao, the Black Wind King, was His Majesty’s most trusted minister in the first place. As a qualified emperor, it is basic common sense for him to create conflicts among his subjects, maintain the balance, and play the game of ‘dogs biting each other’. If Your Majesty can realize that the Star

Glory Federation can be the most excellent hound to help him bite Lei Chenghu, Jin Yuyan, Li Linghai, and the rest of the people who are above his head so that he can spare his hands and feet to achieve his ambitions, believe me, Your Majesty will definitely look at the federation in a different light. Not

only will the safety of the federation be ensured, it is also possible that a lot of resources will be allocated to the federation, allowing it to grow.”

Chapter 2878: Raising War With War

“Is General Di planning to turn the entire federation into the ‘emperor’s party’ of the Imperium of True Human Beings and even include your own Infinite Commerce Alliance?”

Bai Kaixin sneered. “Is this considered betraying the Infinite Commerce Alliance?”

“What if Tam?”

Looking at his fair, fat hands, Di Feiwen smiled and said, “Betrayal is nothing unusual for the Immortal Cultivators, and the federation did not accept me and my subordinates because of my loyalty, did it?

Bai Kaixin was lost for words.

“We’ve been working on the political changes of the imperium, especially the intelligence analysis and personality sketch of the new emperor.

Guo Chunfeng, the veteran intelligence chief of the federation, coughed and continued the conversation unhurriedly. “What General Di said might be what we thought, but it is too dangerous after all. Interfering with the political changes of a major nation with the capability of a small nation is like

playing with fire in an abyss. One moment of carelessness, and we will be doomed forever!”

“We are playing with fire. But so what? Hasn’t the Star Glory Federation been playing with fire since it was born?

“You were playing with fire when you established the ‘Nine Sect Alliance’ in the harsh environment where demons were everywhere. After the Far East Demon Kingdom was destroyed, the war between the Heaven’s Origin Sector and the Blood Demon Sector was also playing with fire every day. Li Yao’s

defeat of Xiao Xuance in the Flying Star Sector was playing with fire. The human beings and the demons reached a truce. The Heaven’s Origin Sector, the Blood Demon Sector, and the Flying Star Sector were playing with fire when they established a new federation. The new federation was playing with fire

when it fought against the Black Wind Fleet. The rise of the federation over the past six hundred years was built on steel, blood, and fire among mountains of blades, seas of flames, and forests of swords. There was no such thing as a sure victory. There was no such thing as an easy rise. There was no such

thing as a world peace without risks at all.

“It is not the first day that you’ve learned the difference between the capability of the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Star Glory Federation. The Star Glory Federation is a small country in the periphery of the sea of stars that only occupies nine barren worlds and one or two

advanced prehistoric relics. If such a small country wants to compete with the superpowers that include hundreds of Sectors without playing with fire, taking risks, or gambling with all your assets, I don’t know what kind of ‘opportunities’ you are expecting.

“Allin all, for a small country in the periphery such as the federation to maintain a strategic balance with a large country in the sea of stars such as the imperium, they must intervene in the internal situation of the imperium and ensure that the imperium is ina state of factions and strife. Then, they must

pull in some factions and fight some, making themselves feared by all parties. All parties have no choice but to fight for the ‘third faction’ that is beyond the situation. Isn’t this the fundamental national policy of the federation for the past hundred years?

“No matter how it is implemented, the ultimate military might of the federation must be sent to the imperium first. The Immortal Cultivators believe in the rule that ‘the strong prey on the weak, and the winner takes all’. Nothing can be achieved without demonstrating the invincibility of the federation

first. No deal can be made!

The experts of the federation fell into a brief silence.

After a moment of silence, more heated debates took place.

With Di Feiwen’s words as the foundation, the experts of the Ancient Saint World were finally able to express their opinions. Their opinions might not be very organized, but at the very least, it was enough to let the leadership of the federation know that, in the federation, there was still a market for the

pro-war arguments.

It did make sense now that he thought about it. Over the past hundred years, under the threat of the Black Wind Fleet, the war machine had been operating at a relatively high efficiency. The construction of society and the advancement of civilization had always been focused on the army and the armed

forces. The soldiers and the strong enjoyed a high social status.

At that time, few people believed that the invasion of the Black Wind Fleet would be resolved easily. Three to five generations of people had been prepared to fight until there were no survivors. The entire federation had been burnt to the ground. People had crawled into the crust and fought for another

five hundred years!

Right now, the threat of the Black Wind Fleet had been resolved by the sacrifices of Li Yao and countless other federal soldiers.

However, the hundred-year-long state of quasi-war and the iron-willed personality that had been shaped by the shadow of war for generations were not so easy to restore to a peaceful state.

Putting everything else aside, the military and military expenses of the federation, which were several times higher than normal, were astronomical.

Should the army and the starships, which had been prepared for the final war, continue?

If the army was not maintained on such a large scale, the resettlement of the servicemen and the sealing and scrapping of the starships would lead to tremendous economic, social, and even political problems. Countless rebellions during the rise and fall of dynasties had resulted in tragedies because the

resettlement of the servicemen was not handled well.

If it had to be maintained, where would the money come from? It must be noted that not only was every starship a giant beast that could swallow gold, every Cultivator was a bottomless hole that could swallow resources. The daily food expenses of all the warriors and Cultivators were enough to make the

chief of staff of the federal army happy and worried.

Once the war machine was fully functioning, it was impossible to slow it down. Only by filling enough flesh, blood, and bones would it be able to brake.

In the defense of the federation, not enough flesh, blood, and bones had been added, which was the biggest problem.

“Wars bum money. Burning one’s own money is a loser, while burning other people’s money is a winner.”

Or rather, the eagles on the prairie of the Ancient Saint World, or rather, the leader of the bandits on the prairie, Han Baling, declared, “For the long-lasting peace of the federation, the current size of the army must be maintained or expanded. Disarmament is out of the question.

“The federal finances could not sustain such a large-scale army. A few years ago, faced with the threat of death, the citizens were naturally willing to contribute all their resources and grit their teeth through the toughest years. But right now, is there any reason for them to do so?

“If we don’t have enough funds and we have to maintain such a large-scale army, what other options do we have other than to expand and win again and again?

“There’s no time to lose. Let’s go to the center of the sea of stars right now. On one hand, we will be supported by the resources and technology of the new imperium. On the other hand, we will be able to plunder the wealth of the surviving families of the four electors. We will even have the opportunity to

take over the Greater Thousand Worlds controlled by the four major families and the Covenant Alliance and make a fortune from the war. With the immeasurable war money, we will be able to release war bonds within the federation. The stocks of the major sects and groups will be a hot topic, too. All the

citizens will be willing to dig out their trump cards and help the federal government survive the most difficult times.

“What can be a better deal than this?”

“The prerequisite for making a fortune in a war is to win before winning!

Bai Kaixin retorted, “Can we guarantee our victory?”

“Of course not.”

“If we fail, the federation will be destroyed. No war bonds or extravagant promises will be fulfilled. Nobody will care about them anymore, will they?”

Such irresponsible remarks naturally raised a new round of rebuttals. The pro-war advocates and the pro-war advocates were fighting back and forth. The atmosphere was getting more and more intense, and the focus of the two parties had drifted away.

At this moment, the sound of an ebony walking stick hitting the deck came from the end of the conference table.

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

The debate came to an end. Everybody looked at the old man who was covered in moss and looked like a rock.

Although he was the most famous old man in the federation,

There was no telling whether or not he was a man of virtue and prestige, but Gui Shishou was indeed the one who had lived the longest in the federation. He was also one of the experts with the highest Cultivation level, almost on par with Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, two spectral Cultivators in the

Divinity Transformation Stage.

However, he concentrated alll his techniques on the skill ‘Health and Longevity’.

Even so, his long life was enough for him to pass countless soul-stirring, unpredictable years and accumulate abundant life experience to guide the juniors in front of him.

Gui Shishou was qualified to participate in the meeting of the experts of the federation and considered all the experts of the federation to be his juniors.

He was even ‘older’ than the Star Glory Federation. Six hundred years ago, before the Nine Sect Alliance on Heaven’s Origin, the predecessor of the Star Glory Federation, was established, Gui Fushou was already used to the vicissitudes of life.

The old demon had always considered himself to be a ‘recorder of history’. He had even devised the ‘Tombstone Plan’ in order to record the most glorious moments of the human beings and the demon race in the periphery of the sea of stars.

Right now, it was the most glorious and dangerous moment of the Star Glory Federation. The duration of his consciousness was much longer than the previous hundreds of years combined. He was even appointed as the vice dean of the ‘Life Research Institute’ of the New Federation, where he studied the

training and longevity of human beings with biochemical technology.

As a matter of fact, the Star Glory Federation, which boasted the last organized force of the demon race in the Pangu Universe, had also received a complete biochemical technology system from the Blood Demon Sector. By combining the biochemical technology of the Blood Demon Sector with the

primeval relics of the Ancient Saint World and the corpses of the Pangu Clan in the Kunlun Mysterious Realm, countless unique, remarkable biochemical training techniques were analyzed and retrieved.

The exploration of the federation in the field of biochemistry was even ahead of that of the Star Ocean Imperium and the Covenant Alliance. In such a regard, Gui Shishou could be called a living database and museum. He had made the greatest contribution.

The Life Research Institute of the Federation’s Spiritual Energy Research Center, which was led by him, had even conducted extensive tracing investigations and data sampling on all the Cultivators in the federation, whether they were Cultivators, Immortal Cultivators, or demons. With countless papers

and research reports, the word ‘cultivation’ had been further standardized, digitized, and reusable, providing clear guidance for the advancement of countless Cultivators.

Many experts of the federation respected Gui Shoushou very much.. They knew that he must have something very important to say—otherwise, this thousand-year-old demon would be too lazy to waste his energy to open his mouth!

Chapter 2879: Glorious Epitaph

“Everyone’s words just now are very reasonable. The problems that you are worried about are very likely to happen, too. I’m not the best at weighing the military and the political issues. It’s just that, from what I’ve heard, the only thing that matters is the conflicts of interest. But I’ve rarely heard you talk

about the ‘great Dao’. Then, let’s talk about the ‘great Dao’ and the ‘belief of the federation!”

Gui Yishou held his cane and lowered his head, both as a rock on the seashore and as a rusted bell. He tapped everybody’s head with his words. Pondering for a moment, he said slowly, “To be honest, it does not seem appropriate for an old man such as myself to talk about the Cultivation of the federation.

Besides, the so-called ‘belief is mysterious and unpredictable. Countless experts and scholars have studied it for hundreds of years, but nobody has ever figured out what the ‘belief is exactly.

“But does the ‘belief really not exist? Is it just a self-deceiving illusion? A strong self-suggestion? I’m afraid you can’t say that.

“Here, I have the latest report of the Life Research Institute. It is the result of tracing the training and upgrade data of thousands of experts over the past hundred years and analyzing them in great detail.

While the meeting that would decide the fate of the federation and even the future of the human civilization was being held in the meeting room of the super warship Kunlun in the dockyard of the space ring, in Li Yao’s hometown, Meng Xiaolang, an excellent graduate of the best military school in the
