They passed by an alley, where some young high school students were talking to each other. On the glass walls of the skyscrapers, nine out of ten commercials

were about Li Yao’s miracle in the center of the sea of stars. The details of the battle between Li Yao and Wu Yingqi were not transmitted back from the center of the sea of stars, which gave the merchants and the media unlimited room for imagination. Everybody was racking their brains to infer how Li

Yao defeated Wu Yingqi. After repeated exaggeration, the battle had basically been upgraded to the battle between the Supreme Emperor and the Blood God ten thousand years ago.

Looking at the 3D light beams where the image of ‘Federation’s God of War, Federation’s Father, and Federation’s Miracle’ was condensed, Meng Xiaolang smiled, as if he were hiding a secret that only he knew.

“Brother, big brother!”

A few middle-school students noticed him, but they could not tell the difference between him and a serviceman. They crowded around him and asked in a friendly tone, “Are we going to march into the center of the sea of stars? We are going to win, aren’t we? We are going to beat the crap out of the big

bad guys of the imperium, just like we did to the Black Wind Fleet!”

Meng Xiaolang naturally knew that, even if the federation went on an expedition, it was not necessarily to fight against the ‘big bad guys of the imperium’. It was also possible that they would form an alliance with the ‘big bad guys of the imperium to fight against the ‘big bad guys of the imperial election’

and the ‘big bad guys of the Covenant Alliance’!

However, even his friends in the military school might not be able to explain the complicated political problems, let alone the hot-blooded kids!

Meng Xiaolang could only nod his head. He waved his fists and said confidently, “Of course. The federal army is invincible!”

It was also what Meng Xiaolang and most of his fellow students were thinking.

The Black Wind Fleet was already one of the toughest fleets of the Imperium of True Human Beings, but it had been wrecked by the federation.

Although there were more formidable enemies than the Black Wind Fleet, the federation today had made great progress compared with a few years ago, especially the Kunlun Fleet.

‘When he thought of ‘Kunlum Fleet’, Meng Xiaolang felt that his eyes and heart were burning.

Getting rid of the middle-school students, he sped up and turned into an old residential area.

The gray residential area was out of place for the splendid skyscrapers nearby. There had been plans to renovate or even rebuild the place, but most of the residents here were disabled veterans who had been living in the neighborhood for decades and were not willing to move away.

Ina corner of the old residential area, an inconspicuous barbershop was hidden. There was no sign, only half of the door. A three-colored light that had stopped rotating for a long time was hanging on the door. Beside the barbershop, two crooked lines of words were written: “Shaving Head 20, veteran

half price.”

It was an old-fashioned salon that could almost be called a hair salon.

Not to mention nowadays, all kinds of colorful, fancy “beauty centers” and “appearance-designing clubs” could be found everywhere. One could even change their face after having their hair cut. Even in the federation a century or two ago, it was rare to find such… rustic hairdressers.

The store seemed to have been sealed in the amber of time and transmigrated from a thousand years ago. It was full of vintage and stubbornness.

The customers who frequented the shop were the residents of the old residential area, the disabled veterans.

For some reason, the decoration and the craftsmanship of the owner of the small shop were very suitable for the taste of the veterans. The veterans all said that not only was the price reasonable, but the master’s skills were also extraordinary. There were also many unique skills such as ‘phoenix picking

ears’ that had been lost for hundreds of years. Other than this place, it was impossible to find it anywhere else. Even Floating Spear City’s largest ‘wave image design center’ could not be found.

Some people even claimed that the owner of the ‘Tidal Image Design Center’ was willing to pay a high salary to hire a master to manage the place. He even waited at the gate for three days and three nights, only to be ignored by the master. There was no other explanation for his arrogance!

Just like that, word spread like wildfire. The hair-shaving shop without a name became a place that many veterans of Floating Spear City visited every few days. Not just the residents nearby, even the veterans living in the new development zone liked to come to this place without any trouble. Even if they

didn’t need to cut their hair, just pluck their ears, massage their heads, and talk to their brothers, they would still feel happy.

Meng Xiaolang walked to the entrance of the nameless salon. He stood on his tiptoes and looked inside through the window. Fortunately, there were not many people today. There was only one customer lying on a barber chair, comfortably enjoying the ear-picking skills of the owner, who was also the

only master of the salon.

As for the barber, he was not very tall. His hair was white, indicating that he was quite old. But there were not many wrinkles on his face, and he was extremely focused. His eyes that looked like those of a falcon reminded Meng Xiaolang of the snipers in military schools. His hands were so still that he

would not hesitate to pick up a scalpel. If he were wearing a white coat instead of a white one, he would look exactly like a surgeon!

‘The barber who looked like a surgeon was Meng Jiang, Meng Xiaolang’s grandfather.

Meng Xiaolang had been closest to his grandfather since he was a child. He loved watching his grandfather play with the shaver the most. When he was not even able to talk, he would often stare blankly at his grandfather’s white blade flying and dancing above the customer’s head. One look and he could

watch it for an entire day.

Meng Xiaolang often boasted to the students of the military school that he had learned his marvelous saber skills from his grandfather when he was little.

“Grandpa!”

Meng Xiaolang knew that his grandfather hated to be disturbed when he was working. His grandfather often said that he was like a painter who had picked up a paintbrush when he picked up a razor and that he had to finish it in one go. Therefore, he leaned against the window and waited patiently until

the customer left in satisfaction. Then he threw off his military backpack and jumped into his grandfather’s arms.

“Little Lang, why are you back?”

Meng Jiang beamed with joy. He pinched his grandson and looked at him again and again. “You’re taller, darker, and stronger. Good, good, good. However, it’s not the new year or the holiday. Why did you come back? Were you kicked out of the military school?”

“What is it?”

Meng Xiaolang sniffed. He didn’t know when he had stolen the shaver from Meng Jiang’s hand, but he played with it beautifully and let the shaver flow between his fingers like mercury. “Even if you don’t believe me, you must believe in your excellent genes, okay? It’s not an exaggeration to say that your

grandson is the only one who can pick the fleets. Why would anyone kick me out? I took leave in broad daylight and came to Grandpa as soon as | got off the train!”

“Yes. Sneaky. Something’s wrong.”

Meng Jiang’s smile grew even more intense. “What’s wrong? You haven’t even met your parents, and you want to meet your grandfather first?”

“Of course. Grandpa is the eternal idol in my grandson’s heart. How can I not come and see you first? Come, Grandpa, try it. This is a can of ‘Sand Bamboo Shoots Frozen’ that our dormitory’s second strongest student brought back from the Crystal Sector. It is said to be a specialty of the Crystal Sector’s

desert and is the most fresh and tender. I fought with these few animals until my nose was swollen and my face was swollen. Only then was I able to snatch back a can. I didn’t even leave it for my father, so I’m giving it to you!”

‘Meng Xiaolang held the can with both hands and paused for a moment. He observed Li Yao’s expression and said cautiously, “Well, besides, I was thinking of going home with you to see my parents

In the beginning, Meng Jiang was still beaming with joy as he enjoyed his grandson’s filial respect. But when he heard his grandson mention his son, his old face suddenly sank. The age spots on his face condensed like dark clouds as he said, “Why did you bring him up all of a sudden? This soul-stirring

thing is all about money. Last time I came here, he was already beaten out. You want me to meet him? In your dreams!”

“Hey—”

Meng Xiaolang was dumbfounded, but since he had already spoken, he could only force himself to continue. “Don’t—don’t be angry, Grandpa. How did my father offend you?”

“And what about it?”

Meng Jiang angrily said, “Isn’t the crap he’s done enough? You say, come come come. You say. You be the judge. I entrusted the entire ‘Tidal Image Design Center’ that I’d painstakingly worked on for my entire life to him. I thought with all my heart that he would be able to bring my life’s work to greater

heights. In the end, this bastard did what he had to do. He dismissed all my old friends who had been fighting under the heavens with me for my entire life. He came up with an ‘automatic’ design. When the guests came, he would lock them in the so-called ‘Model Cabin’ that he had been fiddling with. Pa!

Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! Pa! He’s done whatever he wanted in the blink of an eye. The entire process was controlled by a crystal processor. Over a hundred spirit prosthetics were completed. There was simply no need to do anything manually.

“It doesn’t need to be man-made. Listen to it. This is the specialty of our ‘Tidal Image Design Center’. Does such a design have a soul? Does it have a life? Does it have a ‘belief? With just this, he dares to double the price? Isn’t he just digging into money? What is this?”

“Yes, yes, yes.”

Meng Xiaolang had no choice but to nod. “I think so, too. Well, what my father is doing right now has no soul, no life, and no… belief. It’s all about money.”

“There’s something even more outrageous. That so-called ‘A Hundred Changes Paper’ as long as you stick it to your forehead and connect it to your portable crystal processor, you can change your appearance with a single thought. Your hair can change into red, orange, yellow, green, blue, blue, and

purple at your will, but it’s not real. It’s just a 3D phantom. It’s just an illusion! I hated this kind of thing the most back in the day. Your father never refused it, either. He became their… ‘strategic partner’ and promoted it in all the stores under the name of ‘Tide Flag’.”

Meng Jiang slapped his thigh: “Isn’t this deceiving the consumers!?”

Chapter 2883: The Way Of Friends

“Exactly, exactly, exactly deceiving the consumers!”

Meng Xiaolang smiled mischievously. “But Grandpa, please don’t be angry. My father is my father, and I am myself. This time, you have to do your grandson a favor.”

“Oh?”

Meng Jiang smiled. “I knew it. You must be up to no good. Tell me, what happened?”

“Well, as I told you last time, the ‘major competition’ for the graduates of our batch is about to begin. If we perform well, it is very likely that we will be selected to join the Burning Prairie Fleet.

Meng Xiaolang paused for a moment. He stuck out his chest and grinned. “I’m in!”

“This is a good thing! I knew it!”

Meng Jiang was both surprised and delighted. “The Burning Prairie Fleet is the best of the best in the federal army. Why don’t you go home and tell your parents such a happy event? Do you want Grandpa to help you?”

“It’s not like that. II told you not to be angry. You were chosen, but your grandson’s performance was too outstanding. Now, the higher-ups have given your grandson another choice.”

Meng Xiaolang lowered his head and said, “If I have the chance, I will go to… another fleet.”

“What do you mean? The Burning Prairie Fleet is already the strongest ace army of the federal army. Didn’t you always say that you might as well pack up and go home if you can’t go to the Burning Prairie Fleet after you go to the military school?”

Meng Jiang was suspicious. “What are you up to again? Is there a better place than the Burning Prairie Fleet?”

“Well—”

Meng Xiaolang shook his head. “I can’t say.”

Meng Jiang narrowed his eyes and looked at his grandson for a long time. He thought for a moment and asked, “Then, where is your new fleet going?”

“I don’t know.”

Meng Xiaolang shook his head again. “I can’t tell you even if I do.”

“Oh. Guess again, Grandpa.”

Meng Jiang said, “Your new fleet must be much more dangerous and arduous than the Burning Prairie Fleet, right?”

“still can’t say.”

Meng Xiaolang said, “Grandpa, I’m sorry. I can’t say anything, but I still hope that you can help me plead with my parents and ask them to let me go. You know, when I applied for the military school, they grumbled for an entire year. If they knew that I went to a mysterious new fleet instead of the Burning

Prairie Fleet, who knows when they would come back, they would definitely agree!”

“Hehe. I see.”

‘Meng Jiang smiled and said, “Are you going to fight a war in the center of the sea of stars with real weapons?”

Meng Xiaolang gasped.

Although he didn’t say anything, his wide-opened eyes and shocked expression betrayed everything.

He almost blurted out, “Grandpa, how did you know?”

“Nonsense. Do you really think your grandfather is old?”

Meng Xiaolang didn’t open his mouth, but Meng Jiang knew what the kid was going to say. He took his razor from his grandson’s hand and scratched it repeatedly, while he mumbled, “Right now, there are loudspeakers shouting ‘Wa la la la’ every day outside. News is changing one after another. They are

all about the expedition to the center of the sea of stars. Also, the regular customers in your grandpa’s shop have been soldiers for decades. If they can’t figure out the market, what can they do?”

“Then—”

Meng Xiaolang felt that something was bothering him. His cheeks were burning, but when he noticed that his grandpa was not anxious or angry, he asked carefully, “Then, then, Grandpa, what if—what if I really go to the center of the sea of stars? Will you support me?”

“Let’s put the support aside for now. Why do you have to go?”

‘Meng Jiang put away the razor neatly. He secretly took out a small bottle of shaving foam from the secret compartment at the bottom of the tool cabinet. He opened the bottle, only to be greeted by the fragrance of wine.

He narrowed his eyes and took a deep breath. Then he took a sip of the canned food that his grandson had brought back and gestured to his grandson. “Go and wait at the gate. Send a signal to your grandpa when you see your grandma enter the neighborhood. Yes. Just squat there and tell your grandpa

what you were thinking. Why did you have to risk your life in the center of the sea of stars instead of living a comfortable life at home?”

“What—what is there to think about? Our Federation has always respected martial strength. Floating Spear City is the most popular place for martial arts in the entire Federation. It’s the ‘Vulture City’. Which Floating Spear man doesn’t want to fight for glory with every strike?”

Meng Xiaolang made up his mind. He held his head high and said, “Grandpa taught me since I was a little kid that, no matter what I do, I should always do my best. Now that I’ve studied in a military school, it’s only natural that I join the best fleet and become the best soldier! I thought that the Burning

Prairie Fleet was the best in the federation, and that’s why I always wanted to join the Burning Prairie Fleet. But right now, I have a better choice. Normal people won’t have the opportunity to join it even if they want to. The elimination rate is more than 99%. There’s no reason for me to waste such a

once-in-a-lifetime opportunity after going through so much trouble to pass the test!

“Besides, besides—”

“Not to mention what?”

Meng Jiang stared at his grandson.

“Besides, I want to help you, grandpa!”

Meng Xiaolang took a deep breath. Seeing that nobody was around, he clenched his fists and declared resolutely, “I’m going to let everyone know that ‘Meng Jiang’ is not only Li Yao’s classmate but also ‘Meng Xiaolang’s’ grandfather. You have a super awesome grandson who will definitely make great

achievements in the center of the sea of stars. He will shine brilliantly and have many fortuitous encounters. He will become the new generation of the Federation’s God of War and the legend of the next hundred years. Hahahaha!”

It was true that Meng Xiaolang had a great secret since childhood—he was the ‘Supreme of the Three Realms, Vulture Li Yao’, who was regarded as a glory and a miracle by the entire federation. He was a hero who had created countless glories and legends. He was also the symbol of Floating Spear City. He

and his blood grandfather were actually classmates in high school!

However, at that time, his grandfather and Li Yao did not seem to be on good terms. He had vaguely heard his grandfather mention that the two of them had some kind of conflict. His grandfather had even… bullied Li Yao!

Therefore, his grandfather did not like to mention the name ‘Li Yao’ at home. Occasionally, when Meng Xiaolang’s father mentioned it, his grandfather would glare at him with a fierce look on his face.

Ever since Meng Xiaolang had become sensible, he had always had a very strange feeling. It was the weird feeling that… although he was a historical figure recorded in textbooks, he had been involved with the people closest to him.

For some reason, he also had the ambition of “If I can do it, then there’s no reason why I can’t do it.”

Besides, it sometimes occurred to Meng Xiaolang that, if his grandfather had really had a conflict with ‘Vulture Li Yao’ when he was young, his grandfather must’ve been very depressed, because his grandfather would never be able to redeem himself when faced with an unparalleled expert such as ‘Vulture

Li Yao’.

It didn’t matter. If Grandpa couldn’t find a place, he would ask his grandson to do it for him. One day, he, Meng Xiaolang, would create a greater glory than ‘Vulture Li Yao”!

Meng Xiaolang blushed. Embarrassed, he told his grandfather everything that had happened.

He did not expect that Meng Jiang would suddenly burst into laughter after staring at him for a long time. He was laughing so hard that he almost spilled the wine contained in the shaving foam.

“Hahahaha. Good grandson. What a good grandson. Grandpa is to blame. Haha. Hahahaha. Grandpa is really to blame!”

Meng Jiang laughed for a long time before he was stopped by the stunned look in his grandson’s eyes. He took a few deep breaths, only to find that his face had tured blue. It was not until half a day later that he winked at his grandson and gestured for him to come over. “Come here. I’ll tell you a secret. If

you are planning to go to the center of the sea of stars because you want to fight for your grandpa and surpass Vulture Li Yao, then it’s unnecessary.

“Grandpa and Li Yao were indeed high school classmates, but there were no conflicts or grudges between them. On the contrary, our relationship was very good at that time. We were best friends.

“Even grandpa went to Li Yao’s wedding. Also, after he returned from the Ancient Saint World after a hundred years and stopped the invasion of the Black Wind Fleet, he didn’t stop contacting grandpa. So, don’t think too much about it!”

“”What—what?”

Meng Xiaolang was dumbfounded. He felt that the world was collapsing in front of him. He stammered, “Grandpa and Li Yao are best friends? How is that possible? I remember clearly that Grandpa and Dad said that you were in a bad relationship at that time. Grandpa even asked someone to beat Li Yao

up, only to be slapped in the face by Li Yao!”

“That was a lie.”

Meng Jiang blinked his eyes and said, “You know your father’s personality better than anyone else. As I said just now, he’s all about money and nothing else! He has been thinking hard about how to make use of the fact that his father and Li Yao are high school classmates to expand his business. If he

learns that his father and Li Yao are best friends, won’t it be a disaster? If he really dares to pester Li Yao and ask him to be the spokesperson of our store, will you believe it if I say that he will help him promote the ‘Automatic Image Design Cabin’ and the ‘Up to One’s Will?”

“Li Yao is actually a very casual person. Based on my grandfather’s friendship with him, even if he is asked to promote the hair growing drug in our store, I estimate that it would not be a problem. However, this is my father’s friendship. How could I let that dog waste it like this? I might as well tell him

that my grandfather has a grudge with Li Yao and completely cut off his hopes. Haha. You didn’t see the expression on his face when I said this for the first time. He was so heartbroken that his eyebrows were about to fall off!”

Meng Xiaolang was rendered speechless. He couldn’t understand his father and grandfather’s weird brain circuits at all.

“Why didn’t you tell me such a big thing earlier? I’ve been gritting my teeth and longing to help you!”

Meng Xiaolang complained, “It’s one thing to keep it a secret from my father, but there’s no reason for you to keep it a secret from your own grandson, is there?”

“In Grandpa’s hundred years of life, this is just a trivial matter. What’s there to talk about?”

Meng Jiang said with a smile, “I am Li Yao’s best friend. I am also Meng Yuan’s son. I am Yu Siyum’s husband, Meng Bo’s father, Meng Xiaolang’s grandfather, and the founder of the “Tidal Image Design Center’. In my heart, the later identities are far more important than the first identity. Do I have to brag

about my friends when I am faced with my own grandson?”

Chapter 2884: 100% Of Life!

“That’s not what I meant!”

Meng Xiaolang blushed and said, “I just thought—I just thought—Wow, let me calm down a bit. In other words, if Grandpa invites Li Yao, this ‘Supreme of the Three Realms, Legend of the Federation’ might really come to our house for dinner?”

“Not ‘possible’. I’ve been here before. This place, of course, not your father’s splendid mansion.”

Meng Jiang said with a smile, “It’s just that Li Yao has stayed in the hibernation chamber for almost a hundred years. In terms of age, he is almost a hundred years younger than me. With my gray hair, I’m more than qualified to be his grandfather. It’s kind of… weird when we get along.”

“Wow!”

Meng Xiaolang pictured Vulture Li Yao having dinner in his grandpa’s inconspicuous salon. Maybe Vulture Li Yao was squatting right where he was. He gave up thinking and did not know what to say. “Wow!”

“What’s wrong? Don’t you hate our ‘legend of the federation’ the most? You’ve been clamoring to surpass him and be ten times better than him.

Meng Jiang intentionally asked, “How come your attitude took a 180 degree turn today?”

“That’s because I thought that Li Yao was Grandpa’s ‘unconquerable enemy’. That’s why I always wanted to avenge Grandpa. Otherwise, who would despise the great heroes of the federation for no reason? They are the symbols of our hometown!”

Meng Xiaolang was all smiles at first, but he suddenly thought of something and frowned again. He said nervously, “Not good. If Li Yao and Grandpa don’t have any conflicts and I don’t need to avenge him or avenge him, will Grandpa still support me to go to the center of the sea of stars?”

“You must ask yourself that question. Ask yourself, why did you join the army?”

Meng Jiang looked at Meng Xiaolang and said calmly, “Is it for money and status, or is it thrilling and thrilling, or is it because you want to go with the crowd, or is it because you are competitive and want to go when other people are not?”

“Well—”

Meng Xiaolang hesitated, deep in thought.

“In fact, you don’t need to ask. Grandpa has been thinking about the question for a long time. Over the past half month, the propaganda outside has been getting livelier and livelier. It seems that the federation is likely to be engaged in a new war. Even if you don’t join the mysterious new fleet but join the

Burning Prairie Fleet or other ordinary fleets, you are likely to be involved in the swirl of war.

Meng Jiang pondered for a moment and said, “Although your father was too embarrassed to come and see me, your mother sent me quite a few spiritual crane messages, asking me to give you a chance to persuade you not to serve in the dangerous army. They knew that you would only listen to me.

“However, hehe, you, your parents, and you, as well as the people on the street outside whose blood is boiling and whose spirits are low, are all new-generation citizens of the federation. Your grandfather, on the other hand, is an old fart from the ‘Old Federation’ of the Heaven’s Origin Sector.

New-generation and old-generation citizens of the federation are different. Too different. You will never understand our mentality, and our understanding of ‘war’ is vastly different.

“The new generation of the federation has grown up ina safe, stable, and prosperous environment. You’ve never experienced the feeling of imminent doom. You’ve experienced a hundred years of peace. Even during the ‘Sky Ring War’ thirty years ago and the recent ‘Federation Defense War’, the flames of

war only swept across space and were limited to the federal army and the Cultivators. It did not affect the people living on the planet much.

“Therefore, you people of the ‘New Federation’ tend to regard wars with unrealistic romance and exaggerated panic. You are too… childish, like children who cannot control their emotions and do whatever they want.

“In short, you don’t have the pain of war. You don’t know what it feels like to have a burning butcher’s knife stabbed into your internal organs.

“What about the Old Federation a hundred years ago?

“It must be hard for you to imagine that we only had one planet named Heaven’s Origin at that time, which was full of wormholes that were connected to the Blood Demon Sector. There was no difference between the front line and the rear line at all, especially on the Grand Desolate Plateau, where

overwhelming beast tides could appear at any moment and engage the brutal enemies at any moment.

“In such a harsh environment, we have no time to consider the gains and losses, the timing and price of the expedition, the strength of the enemy, and the conditions for negotiation like the people of the New Federation do today. Those things don’t exist. Cultivators, soldiers, and civilians are all the

same. Just stick your head in your pocket and do it!

“At that time, it was easier for a person to die than to die for an ant. The destruction of a city was no different from the withering of a weed. Even our civilization, the Star Glory Federation, which had been struggling in blood and flames for five hundred years, could perish at any moment.

“Faced with all this, we have neither the time nor the energy to panic, to hesitate, and to weigh the pros and cons. We can only move forward numbly and eliminate all the enemies that stand in our way. Maybe one day, we will be killed by the enemy. Survival or destruction is not a big deal.

“Tm saying all this because I want to tell you that I will absolutely not stop you from going to the battlefield just because of your safety. We, the people of the ‘Old Federation’, have long grown indifferent to life and death. Even the destruction of the Federation is something that has occurred to us

countless times over a hundred years ago. It’s still the same old saying. We live and work. We die. It’s no big deal.

“But I still hope that you can think clearly, think calmly for a few days, listen to the voice in the deepest part of your heart, and find your own answer. Why do you want to go to the battlefield? What kind of person do you want to be? What kind of life do you want to live?

“Grandpa…”

‘Meng Xiaolang didn’t know what to do. He didn’t expect his grandfather to be so serious all of a sudden.

“To tell you the truth, when I was the same age as you, I was once confused, too. Speaking of which, I was a bit influenced by ‘Vulture Li Yao’—when you have a high school buddy like Li Yao, it’s hard for you not to be disturbed and be influenced by him.”

Meng Jiang pursed his lips into a smile and said, “Although I’m a little embarrassed, Grandpa still has to tell you. At that time, Grandpa was very envious. He was even jealous of ‘Vulture Li Yao.”

“Well…”

Meng Xiaolang took a breath. He thought for a moment and nodded. “Alright. If I were Li Yao’s high school classmate, I’m afraid I would be jealous of him, too.”

“Jealousy is jealousy, but our relationship is still very good. Besides, I know that my talent is limited, and I can’t compete with him in the path of Cultivation. So, I’ve decided that I must be the best in my own business and be the best in the federation. That way, I will be able to hold my head high in front

of him.”

Meng Jiang took another sip of wine and let the hot and spicy taste return to his throat. He said in a low voice, “You probably know what happened later. After I struggled day and night, my business grew bigger and bigger. The brand of ‘Wave’ expanded to the entire federation and even across several

Sectors. We even had franchises on the most remote resource planets. I was getting closer and closer to my goal, but I neglected my family, and the distance between me, my wife, and my children grew larger and larger.

“Over the past few years, your grandma, your father, and I were in a bad relationship. In fact, it’s hard to say whether or not your father was influenced by my early years. But I was still obsessed with the vows I made to my best friend.

“It was not until your grandma was seriously ill and was on the brink of death for an entire year that I came back to myself and realized my mistake.

“What is the most important thing in my life? Is it just a promise with my best friend and not the people around me? Is it more important to hold my head high in front of my best friend than the happiness of my family? Besides, Li Yao is not that kind of person at all. He doesn’t care how big my career is.

Whether or not I am the ‘best image designer of the federation’, we will always be best friends! I—I am not competing with Li Yao at all. I am competing with myself, my mental devil, my vanity, my inferiority, and my jealousy. In the end, I almost lost my wife and my children!

“From that day on, I was enlightened.

“How Li Yao lived his life had nothing to do with me. The first label of my life was ‘Meng Jiang’, not ‘Li Yao’s best friend’. Li Yao had his adventures, and I had mine. My adventures included meeting your grandma, your aunt, and your father, who were… dogs, and of course, your sister and you.

“Compared to the fortuitous encounter that the heavens bestowed upon Li Yao, | feel that this fortuitous encounter bestowed upon me by the heavens is even more precious. I already have my own fortuitous encounter, so why must I compete with Li Yao?

“After I realized this, a new chapter of my life began. Although my career didn’t reach new heights, I can proudly say that I lived every day honestly and happily. I didn’t let down the fortuitous encounter that the heavens bestowed upon me. I cherished my family and enjoyed my current happy life. I lived

my true self. I achieved 100% Meng Jiang, just like Li Yao lived 100% Li Yao.

“Do you understand? You are Grandpa’s most beloved grandson. Grandpa supports all your decisions. I only hope that you don’t act on impulse, that you are not swayed by the media’s propaganda, that you are acting for some ridiculous reason like ‘become a super hero ten times stronger than Li Yao’, or

Chapter 2885: Super Player!

Meng Xiaolang couldn’t help but say, “However, wow. Grandpa, you don’t know this, but when you said those words just now, your entire body was shining. It’s needless to say how handsome you are. more and more curious about you.”

His grandson’s question put Meng Jiang in deep thought.

The depths of the old man’s eyes were dirty at first, before starry brilliance appeared in them. In the end, the starry brilliance condensed into a stream and gradually turned clear again.

“did. Of course I did. Who wouldn’t want to have a life as thrilling as Li Yao’s when they were young and frivolous? Besides, the fact that they were once in the same class as Li Yao easily gave people the illusion that Li Yao was merely lucky. If it were them, they would be just like him.”

Meng Jiang said, “However, as I grew older, I accumulated my life over the long years. After having countless beautiful memories, my thoughts gradually faded away.

But if that was the case, it would be impossible for me to meet your grandma, and it would be impossible for me to give birth

to your aunt and your father. Although your father makes people angry all day long, he was still very cute when he was a child. He was like a lump of soft jelly, rubbing against me all day long. Hahahaha. This dog.”

Meng Xiaolang exclaimed, “If grandpa had become a powerful Cultivator, he wouldn’t have met grandma and fallen in love with her. There wouldn’t have been a father, nor would there have been me!”

“Yes. If that were the case, you and your sister wouldn’t have existed. The good and the bad days of the past hundred years would’ve been gone. The ‘Meng Jiang’ right now was dead. Gone. No. He never existed.

Staring at himself in the wine, Meng Jiang was silent for a long time, as if he had returned to a beautiful place in the distant past.

Acharming smile appeared on his face one moment, and a warm happiness the next. Even his eyes were glittering. He mumbled, “So, exchanging my life for Li Yao’s life with everything I have right now, and even becoming a legend of the federation? The price I have to pay is too high.”

“Grandpa…”

Meng Xiaolang felt that his nose was sour, as if he was brought back to his childhood when he buried himself in his grandfather’s arms like a jelly.

“No.”

‘Meng Jiang said in a low voice. He finished the wine in his cup in one gulp. There was a clicking sound in the depths of his throat. Every pore on his body was emitting a red glow. He shook his head and said, “No, no!”

“Grandpa?”

Meng Xiaolang pulled Meng Jiang’s sleeve.

“Don’t pull me. If I say I won’t trade, then I won’t trade. Let alone one Li Yao, I won’t trade even if a hundred Li Yao’s lives are traded.”

Meng Jiang was a bit drunk. He narrowed his eyes and shook his head. “You want to trade for such a beautiful life and such a beautiful wife… Dream on!”

“No, Grandpa. I meant—”

Meng Xiaolang said, “Grandma is back. I was talking to you and forgot to watch the door. Look, look at the window.”

“Pa Da!”

Meng Jiang was not paying attention and fell off his rickety chair. The cold sand and frozen bamboo shoots as well as the wine that had hit the foam can were all over the floor. The small salon was immediately filled with the strong smell of alcohol and heat.

Meng Xiaolang and his grandparents had dinner together. After much persuasion, Meng Jiang was finally persuaded to go back and meet his son. Of course, the ‘doggie’ had to be invited to the dinner party.

Seeing that his grandfather was supporting him to go to the battlefield in the center of the sea of stars and that his relationship with his father could be repaired, Meng Xiaolang was greatly relieved. He bid farewell to his grandfather and went home to tell his father the good news.

“Wait. Don’t leave just yet. Help me check first.”

Meng Jiang, on the other hand, brought up a very complicated interface from his portable crystal processor. A delicate invitation card appeared on the 3D hologram. “This was given to me by your sister. Can you help me take a look? What is she doing right now?”

Meng Xiaolang had a sister named ‘Meng Lichuan’, who was much smarter and more promising than him. He was still a graduate of the Federation’s military school and had not been granted an official military rank yet. His sister was only seven years older than him, but she was already a famous ‘super

player’ and a professional player in the ‘Civilization’ game. It was the most popular, coolest, and trendy profession among the young people of the Federation. She was the idol of the entire nation!

You have such a good thing, but you only took it out now. This… This competition has already started for an entire day and night. Aiya, aya!”

Meng Xiaolang was hopping mad.

He had been participating in the fully-enclosed matches in the military school, including the selection of the most confidential ‘Kunlun Fleet’. Naturally, he could not communicate with the outside world.

After the results of the selection of the ‘Kunlun Fleet’ were out, he finally got three days of vacation and rushed home without asking about his sister.

He had learned the news of the so-called “best confrontation that would determine the future of the federation” on the train. He did not expect that his sister would be able to get the invitation code for the live stream. It meant that not only had she advanced to the first team, she was also the main force and even the core of the first team. She was truly awesome!

“Treally don’t understand. Why are you young people so excited? Have you gone mad after seeing the game?”

Meng Jiang frowned and said, “Don’t get me wrong. Your grandfather is not an old fogey who doesn’t know anything. When your grandfather was young, crystal processor games were already popular. Online matches were nothing unusual, including professional games and professional matches.

Your sister is a ‘super

player’. She is also known as the ‘crownless king’. She is even more awesome than the representatives of the parliament. What kind of name is that?”

Meng Xiaolang explained, “As for ‘super players’ such as my sister, they are analyzing the real data with their wisdom and computational ability and inferring the situation of the war and the future so that the authorities can make policy choices and predictions.

“Let’s say that ‘Glorious Expedition’, the latest expansion pack of the ‘Civilization’ game that my sister is fighting in, was authorized by the Dark Moon Foundation and the authorities to send back detailed real data about the Imperium of True Human Beings in order to infer the changes of the federal army

in the center of the universe. Since the military hasn’t imported the latest data yet, it is not hard to imagine how confidential it is.

“Therefore, only super teams with backgrounds such as ‘Team Moon Fiend’ can get in touch with this expansion pack. Not to mention playing with it, even watching the live stream of the match has been edited and hidden. A lot of key data has been hidden, and only the general picture can be seen.

“The invitation code that my sister gave me allows me to see more details and data than the public Spiritual Nexus. It is definitely a priceless treasure!

“Hehe…”

Meng Jiang blushed and mumbled in a low voice, “At the end of the day, it’s just a game.. What’s the big deal? Tell me, how can the ‘crownless king’ change the direction of public opinion and even interfere with the strategical decisions of the authorities?”

Chapter 2886: General Participation In Strategies

“Of course it does!”

Meng Xiaolang said, “In ancient times, people like my sister would’ve been the ‘think tanks’ around the king, and their super teams would’ve been high-level ‘think tanks’. calculate the changes of the situation. If a certain representative entered the parliament because he was so strong that he could blow up a mountain with a single punch, how could he not listen to the opinions of the experts?”

Meng Jiang suddenly realized something. “Just like our Speaker?”

“Yes. She is the wife of your old classmate Li Yao, the famous ‘Dragon of Fire’ of the federation!”

Meng Xiaolang couldn’t help but laugh. “Everybody in the federation knows that Speaker Ding is best known for her strength and charisma. She doesn’t know much about the big picture, but as the Speaker of Parliament of the federation, she doesn’t need to be adept at all fields. Naturally, countless

“Of course, this is just one of them.

“More importantly, it will influence public opinion and allow the general public to understand the current situation and problems in the fastest and easiest way.

reliable when compared with the Black Wind Fleet.

While Meng Xiaolang was talking, he had already brought up the only live arena of ‘Glorious Expedition’, the latest expansion of ‘Civilization’.

Meng Xiaolang was qualified to join the latest ‘Kunlun Fleet’ of the federation. Naturally, his reading ability was a hundred times better than his grandfather’s.

Meng Jiang didn’t think much of it a moment ago. But now, his curiosity was aroused by his grandson. He asked, “Which side won? Your sister’s Team Moon Fiend or the opponent’s Team Blood Shadow? Can you see what your sister is doing from here? Tell me!”

“Well…”

‘As Meng Xiaolang read the match, his face gradually turned pale and awkward.

The serial serial numbers that represented the starships of the federal army turned from bright green to deep green, from deep green to orange, and from orange to crimson. In the end, they were burnt to ash in the burning redness.

In only several minutes, countless starships and soldiers of the federal army had reached the end of their lives.

It seemed that the imperial army sent by the revolutionaries to defend the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the federal army that came to reinforce them had been surrounded by the fleet of the Covenant Alliance. The situation of the battle was extremely bad!

“sh*t!”

At first, Meng Lichuan’s eyes were orange, and then they were filled with paleness. Her soul seemed to have been pushed out of the virtual world by an intense blast and returned to the gaming capsule that was full of neural interactive liquids.

Meng Lichuan suddenly sat up and cursed.

This was the Federal Information Center, the center of ‘Civilization’ in Hundred Flowers City. It was the biggest secret base.

It was also a national-level strategic deduction center that was closely followed by the Secret Sword Department, the Dark Moon Foundation, the military, and the Glorious Sunlight Group.

For the strategical decisions that would determine the future of the federation, the first public strategical deduction of whether or not to march to the center of the sea of stars was to be carried out in this place, which was also known as the ‘Glorious Expedition’.

Among the tens of thousands of array-type super crystal processors, thousands of the most advanced gaming capsules were densely arranged. Those lying inside the neural interaction liquids were all experts of deduction who were one-in-ten-thousand and had the strongest computational ability, the

most meticulous logic, and the most abundant imagination. They were not simply ‘super players’ but the elite intelligence personnel of the Dark Moon Foundation and the precious babies of the military.

Meng Lichuan was one of them.. He was also the well-acknowledged Newcomer King and was very confident that he would become a real ace player in the next ten years!

Chapter 2887: Intentional Murder

As a future ace player, ‘calmness’ was the foundation of the foundation. Meng Lichuan considered himself to be a man who wouldn’t change his attitude even when a planet was about to collapse. Otherwise, he wouldn’t have been able to fight his way through the battles from the clubs in the periphery of

Team Moon Shadow to the young warriors, and then from the young warriors to the third team, the second team, and even to the point where he could represent the first team and compete with the top experts from the military on the peak battlefield.

But right now, although she had been training for almost thirty years, her iron-like nerves were showing signs of breaking down. She was about to curse him without caring about anything else—there was no way that she could win the battle!

‘Meng Lichuan was a talent in operations in the team. To put it simply, he was planning and distributing the assets of the fleet, mainly the distribution of fuel and ammunition.

In the unprecedented virtual war, hundreds of supply ships were directly under her command. Her task was to send the supply ships to where they were needed most safely and quickly.

‘Meng Lichuan was absolutely confident in what he was best at.

Many veteran members of Team One often joked that, with Meng Lichuan’s management, there was no need for any tricks. All they needed to do was sprint forward.

Even the military had tried to poach Meng Lichuan several times, hoping that he could join the federal army. With the talent that she showed in the virtual world, it was not impossible for her to become the real commander of a supply fleet or even the advisor of the commander-in-chief who was

responsible for logistics.

In the battles over the past day and night, Meng Lichuan had performed his talents and hard work to the best of his ability and completed the task that the team assigned to her. The supply ships commanded by her braved the furious attacks of the enemy and moved through the battle lines smoothly,

making sure that the iron beasts that looked like bottomless pits were firing nonstop and launching glamorous attacks like mercury!

Unfortunately.

The tactical advantage was not enough to offset the strategic disadvantage.

The expedition army controlled by Meng Lichuan and her companions was almost flawless in every detail of the battlefield, but they were still unable to resist the tide of destruction of the fleet of the Covenant Alliance.

Under the fearless and even suicidal attacks of the members of the Covenant Alliance, the virtual federal army and the virtual imperial army did not cooperate well. They were wary of each other and even hoped that their ‘friends’ would be killed first. Very soon, the gap between them was discovered by

the members of the Covenant Alliance, and a fatal wound was torn open. The imperial army and the federal army were separated and attacked separately.

Under the furious assault of the enemy, the supply ships under Meng Lichuan’s control were burning and exploding one after another. Even if they arrived next to the arsenals in time, it would still be difficult for them to complete the supply mission in the surging battlefield. Often, they would be buried

together with the arsenals.

Later, even the flagship of Meng Lichuan’s supply fleet was captured. She was forced to withdraw from the battlefield after a series of dazzling fireballs.

What the heck!

Meng Lichuan could not have been more furious. He slapped the neural interaction liquid next to him so hard that the cold mucus was almost ignited by her fury. “How did the director set up the rules of deduction? It’s not fair. The casualty rate of the fleet of the Covenant Alliance is more than 50%, but

the morale of the fleet is not affected at all, and the salvo is not affected, either. Isn’t it outrageous?”

“Don’t be hasty. I’m told that the members of the Star Glory Federation are like that.

“According to the latest intelligence sent back by the Star Ocean Imperium, in the wars between the Star Ocean Imperium and the Star Ocean Imperium, there were even cases where the casualty rate of the fleet of the Star Ocean Imperium reached 70% and yet they did not retreat. After all, the people of

the Star Ocean Imperium were different from normal people. They had no fear, no discouragement, and no fear of death. They were nothing but killing machines that were as cold as steel. You may consider that the people of the Star Ocean Imperium had the talent to ‘lock onto morale’. That was their

specialty!

“Alright. I can accept the fact that the members of the Covenant Alliance can ‘lock onto morale’, but what about us ‘friends’?

Meng Lichuan was still angry. He gnashed his teeth and said, “The morale of the allies of the imperium is too low. They are about to collapse. What’s the big deal about that? Aren’t we helping them defend the capital city? We must show them that we will live or die with the capital city no matter what!”

“Well, there is a good reason for that.

His companion explained to the ‘genius’ who had just joined Team One and did not know what was going on yet. “According to the design of the director team, in fact, the revolutionaries in the deduction are fighting on two fronts. Other than the invasion of the Holy Alliance, they are also faced with the

insurrection of the remaining members of the electors in the rear. Also, their backgrounds are too complicated, and they have not fought for a long time. Their teamwork with us is not very good. It is not strange that their morale is low!

Meng Lichuan took a deep breath and bellowed, “What about us? Why is the morale of our federal expedition army affected by the d*mn setting, too? The entire army was shaken when the casualty rate was less than twenty percent. Are we Cultivators that weak?”

“This is based on the military’s comprehensive understanding of the psychological state of the grassroots soldiers.

The companion continued. “Of course, the psychologists and the Meditation Healers have their own professional judgements. Anyway, according to the mentality of the soldiers at the grassroots level in the military right now, if they encounter a minor setback after they jump to the center of the sea of

stars, their morale will plummet. You know, not everyone is as confident as you are that the final victory will be ours!”

Meng Lichuan was dazed for a long time. He stared at the blinking super crystal processors above his head and could only curse helplessly.

It was true that she was a zealot and an unwavering ‘victor’. She believed that the federation and the Cultivators were invincible. She also believed that she would bum brightly in the ‘greatest war’.

This kind of mentality was perhaps similar to that of his blood brother, Meng Xiaolang, who was trying to fight for his grandfather and help his grandfather get back the face he lost at Vulture Li Yao’s place a hundred years ago.

The poor girl did not know that her grandfather and Li Yao did not have any conflicts and were best friends.

She did not intend to do anything to Li Yao. However, if she could rise to fame and prove that Meng Jiang’s granddaughter was better than Li Yao’s granddaughter, she believed that her grandfather would be very happy.

Of course, Li Yao didn’t seem to have a granddaughter yet. He didn’t even have any children.

Allin all, Meng Lichuan was such a confident man.

But today, for the first time in her life, she had tasted the taste of a crushing defeat, and it was so frustrating!

Strategical deduction was not a real war after all. It was impossible to summon tens of thousands of professionals to participate. Therefore, the super players could still return to the battlefield with new identities after ‘unfortunate deaths’.

However, he would be punished with a certain amount of cooldown time.

Meng Lichuan’s cooldown was fifteen minutes.

According to the flow of time according to the strategy, it would be a few hours on the battlefield.

She silently operated her mental cultivation technique, allowing her spiritual energy to slowly flow along her cerebrovascular vessels to cool down her brain, which was about to explode. In passing, she opened the “Glorious Expedition, Summit Confrontation” forum and browsed through the public’s

comments regarding the battle. Just a moment ago, in the sea of stars where the flames of war were raging, every brain cell was stuffed with battlefield data. How could she have the time to care about these things?

As she expected, the Moon Shadow Team, which represented the expedition army of the federation, had performed terribly. Everybody was greatly shocked.

Before the match began, the forum had been filled with positive and optimistic voices. The public believed that, even if the federal army could not win the match easily, it could at least help the revolutionaries defend the defense line of Empyreal Terminus so that the Star Glory Federation and the

Covenant Alliance would be engaged in a contest of flesh and blood. Then, the federal army would decide what to do in the end.

“Things are different now. The federation today has digested most of the technology and knowledge of the Kunlun Mysterious Land. The combat ability of the Burning Prairie Fleet is even higher than that of the Black Wind Fleet. It is rarely matched in the center of the sea of stars!

“That’s right. If the soldiers of the Star Glory Federation were really so strong, they wouldn’t have been unable to break through the defense line for a thousand years. The imperial army alone was enough to stop the soldiers of the Star Glory Federation, not to mention the federal army now!

“I don’t think the strategical deduction is good enough. It is not realistic enough. What we should worry about most is not the people of the Star Glory Federation, but whether or not the imperium will abandon us and shoot us in the back!

“It’s impossible. The battlefield is set in the capital city of the imperium. If we have to fight to the death, we might as well crush the hearts of the citizens of the imperium!”

The great victory over the Black Wind Fleet a few years ago had melted into the bones of the people and become the belief of countless people of the federation. Such blindly confident statements were everywhere.

In the middle of the battle, the morale of the coalition army of the Star Glory Federation, the Star Glory Federation, and the Star Glory Federation fell much faster than anybody expected.

Just as many experts had predicted, arrogance and inferiority were two sides of the same coin. The pride that was inflated by a victory could also be completely extinguished by a defeat or even a failure.

“The people of the Covenant Alliance are so strong? Lunatics. They are a bunch of out-and-out lunatics!”

“Are you kidding me? Isn’t it too exaggerated that the director team is comparing the capability of our enemy?”

“Only a dozen days have passed in the strategical deduction. The capital that the federation has painstakingly accumulated for decades has been lost so much?”

“How are we going to fight the war? No. The federation cannot jump into such a pit of fire without a good reason. We have to think about it carefully. We have to think about the reasons why we have to join the war. By the way, this is not a trap of the ‘reformist Immortal Cultivators’ who are asking us to

be buried with them on purpose, is it? As I said, there are no good people in the Immortal Cultivators. The reformists and the survivors of the election are all jackals of the same tribe. They are all Immortal Cultivators!

Comments such as sighing and gritting their teeth gradually became mainstream on the forum.

Even the polls of several authoritative websites were dropping in the direction of ‘think before you act’. Less than 40% of the people still supported the idea of an expedition to the center of the sea of stars as soon as possible..

Chapter 2888: Unable To Return... To Heaven?

»pamn..”

Every word in the forum stung Meng Lichuan’s eyes like burning nails, pierced through her chest, and burned her palm.

She did not necessarily stand at a high strategic position and firmly believed in principles such as ‘the lips are gone and the teeth are cold’.

It was her natural stubbornness that made her unable to stand failure, especially in the first important strategical deduction that she participated in as a representative of Team Moon Fiend.

Beep! Beep! Beep!

Now that the cooldown was over, she could join the battle again.

Taking a deep breath and focusing her mind and soul, Meng Lichuan sank into the Nerve Interaction Liquid again. But she saw a series of colorful data streams through her closed eyes and entered the strategical deduction again.

This time, she was playing the role of a high-level advisor of a support fleet. She was also responsible for transporting the fuel, ammunition, and space shuttles to the most important places on the frontline. If possible, she would drag the broken starship and the wounded back and repair them in the

large maintenance fortress in the rear. At the very least, thirty to fifty percent of their combat ability would be restored.

For a moment, the torrent of data, the swirl of information, and the meteor shower made of various formulas, graphs, and curves smashed into her brain.

In such a strategical deduction at the level of experts, there were barely any scenes that the ordinary audience liked to see, such as ‘starships blasting each other in a battle line, interconnecting the space shuttles and the crystal suit legions, and the Giant God Soldiers tearing open the stomachs and

crushing everything in their way’, where the war had been simplified or reduced to its original appearance. Countless cold data changes were taking place, each of which represented a starship or thousands of lives. The disappearance of data was even more insignificant than the annihilation of chess

pieces, and Meng Lichuan had to make up his mind not to think about the meaning behind the numbers or the chess pieces and send them to the place where they should die as precisely as possible.

It was not an easy job.

She was under even more pressure than the real staff and commanders of the Star Ocean Imperium.

It was because she was not going to be the only one who had so much data in a real battle in the sea of stars. The strategists, captains, and commanders would share the pressure for her when she commanded so many starships. She only needed to be a simple screw.

In this place, Meng Lichuan had to deal with almost a hundred pieces of information every second and distribute the last batch of assets of the federal army to the frontline precisely.

It was better to have data that could be processed. More often than not, when the enemy launched high-intensity spiritual interference and cut off all her sources of data, she was like a blind horse galloping on a cliff. She could only rely on her ‘intuition’, ‘talent’, and ‘luck’ to decide the distribution of the

assets.

Under the tremendous computational ability and the mental pressure, Meng Lichuan’s brain broke through the limits of fatigue and reached the brink of collapse again in only half an hour.

Ina trance, she could not tell whether she was carrying out a virtual deduction or watching her fleet fall into the abyss of destruction in the cruel battlefield.

The boring data and the complicated diagrams all turned into vivid illusions.

Meng Lichuan seemed to see countless starships of the federation burning at the edge of the Empyrean Terminus Sector into shrieking butterflies.

They also seemed to see bleeding wounds being torn open in the dark space. More, faster, and fiercer starships of the Covenant Alliance were arriving through the wormholes.

Disturbed by the radiation of the star, the invisible waves from the cannons of the two parties were both emitting glamorous and terrifying colors. The rays interweaved, twisted, and intertwined, as if countless mottled, brilliant snakes had appeared in the sea of stars and were biting and swallowing each

other.

The giant snake that represented the federation, on the other hand, was swallowed by the giant snake that represented the Covenant Alliance.

“No. Absolutely not!”

Meng Lichuan gritted his teeth. His computational ability was five times higher than before because of the unknown force. Every supply ship under his command was like her nerve endings that she had deployed precisely.

On the frontline that she was in charge of, the firepower of the federal army was doubled instantly. Countless starships of the Covenant Alliance were drowned in the brilliant ocean of flames. Many experienced teammates sent ‘appreciation’ to the newcomer in the communication channel.

It was a pity that, in the magnificent confrontation of almost a million starships, individual strength was insignificant after all. No matter how valiant Meng Lichuan’s side was, it was impossible to reverse the decline of the other dozens of fleets.

Besides, even a clever housewife cannot cook without rice. When Meng Lichuan ran out of the last batch of assets and all the supply ships under her command turned into empty tanks, there was nothing she could do!

“Lichuan, there’s something wrong with the amplitude of your brainwaves. Quit the deduction now!”

‘Meng Lichuan vaguely heard a voice calling him anxiously.

“Damn it. Damn it. Damn it. How could we have lost? This deduction was simply reckless. Just reckless!”

Meng Lichuan’s eyes were bloodshot. He ignored the warnings and gazed at the nonexistent fleet of the Covenant Alliance through the messy data.

“Lichuan, you are going to be mentally deranged. Quit now!”

The voice continued shouting.

Meng Lichuan, on the other hand, heard the mocking, mocking, screaming, and frustrated sighs on the forum.

“Bullsh*t! We won’t lose! I won’t admit defeat just like that!”

Meng Lichuan gritted his teeth. Every brain cell was boiling and exploding.

“Forced exit. Forced exit!”

The voice in his ear screamed.

‘The data in front of Meng Lichuan’s eyes was jumping crazily, indicating that a fleet of the Star Glory Federation had discovered her small supply fleet.

The acceleration and agility of the enemy’s warship were much higher than those of her carrier. She had nowhere to run to.

Meng Lichuan sniffed. Without any hesitation, he maneuvered the supply ships under his command into a head-on battle formation. However, because of the design of the directing team, the ‘mutual morale’ and ‘commanderliness’ of her fleet had hit rock bottom. It was barely possible for them to

assume a ‘mutual destruction’ stance.

Therefore, before she was ready to die, the enemy had marched into her fleet like a hungry tiger pouncing on a lamb. The figures in front of Meng Lichuan turned into shining shooting stars, which congregated into a bleak white brightness and suffocated her soul. She passed out without even finishing

her curses.

Meng Lichuan had the deepest sleep of his life.

He also had the most terrifying and absurd nightmares.

Most of her nightmares were related to the great war in the sea of stars. She seemed to have fallen into a thick swamp that was full of oil. Then, the oil was ignited, and she watched her and her companions’ heads burning furiously.

The nightmare repeated itself countless times before Meng Lichuan managed to open his bloodshot eyes. He stared at the ceiling for a long time before he was certain that he had woken up.

But she still had a splitting headache, as if somebody had cut open her head and stuffed a starship inside.

‘The smell of disinfectant in the air and the beeping monitors next to her made her certain that she was lying in the med bay of the hospital.

It must’ve been a long time, so long that she thought hard for a while before she remembered what she had been doing.

“Competition! I’m in a competition!”

Meng Lichuan’s heart skipped a beat. He sat up abruptly, only to feel that the world was spinning. The starship inside his head opened fire and smashed her back. His ears were ringing, and he was seeing stars.

“Hey hey hey, what are you doing? Do you want to lie down for another three days and three nights?”

Around, somewhat ‘cute’ face appeared above the med bay and looked at her with a smile.

Meng Lichuan blushed and shouted in embarrassment.

‘Sister Tao’ was named Jiang Baitao. She was a veteran member of ‘Team Moon Fiend’ and a senior who had brought Meng Lichuan into the sect. According to the rules of the world of Cultivators, she was her ‘guide’. Naturally, the two of them were quite close.

“My head hurts. What—what happened to me, Sister Tao?”

‘Meng Lichuan held his temples and took a few deep breaths. “Competition. We are still in the middle of the competition. Why are you here?”

“Thave bad news and good news.”

Jiang Baitao smiled. “The bad news is that you triggered your brain cells without caring about anything else. Your computational ability was so high that your cerebrovascular vessels almost exploded. You fell into the most serious mental derangement and have been lying in the hospital for three days and

“The good news is that ‘almost’ mental derangement does not mean mental derangement. rubbing their fists in preparation to focus on fostering you. Even the military, the Glorious Sunlight Group, and the major sects are very interested in you. “Ah”

‘Meng Lichuan was somewhat embarrassed. His cheeks were slightly red.

“The match—is it over?”

Seeing Jiang Baitao’s cheerful face, Meng Lichuan felt that something was wrong. “Did we win the game by a miracle at the last moment?”

Chapter 2889: : Sisters: Invasion Of Darkness

Meng Lichuan’s head was ina mess.

The future of countless people was in their hands.

That was why Meng Lichuan was trying so hard to complete his mission even at the cost of burning his brain.

In the middle of the battle, the results of the forum proved that the polls were closely related to the progress of the battle.

When ‘Team Moon Fiend’ had a slight advantage, more people supported sending troops. When ‘Team Blood Shadow’ gradually secured an overwhelming victory, more people opposed sending troops.

Right now, Team Moonfiend had suffered a crushing defeat, which meant that the expedition army of the federation had been entirely wiped out. Shouldn’t the polls have plummeted? How come more and more people were supporting the decision to send troops? It was a one-sided situation.

ister Tao!”

Chapter 2890: Reverse Psychology

Meng Lichuan was rendered speechless. He quickly browsed through the forums and posts related to ‘Darkness Invasion’.

As she expected, many people were as shocked and terrified as her about the new expansion pack.

Chapter 2891: Silent Loyalty

Chapter 2892: Our History, Your Future!

‘Meng Lichuan was dazed.

But the concept of ‘underground natives’ was completely different.

In the end, human beings were creatures who lived on the ground and in the light. Even if they lived in starships for the rest of their lives, they would still try to simulate the blue sky and the white clouds in order to provide sufficient sunlight.

Meng Lichuan found it hard to imagine how miserable it must have been for a human being to live for ten thousand years in an extremely harsh environment tens of thousands of meters below the ground and close to the mantle.

‘The high temperature and pressure brought by billions of tons of rocks were enough to crush the body of a human being and their free will into powder.

‘The few days of gaming experience in the ‘Inferno of Darkness’ expansion pack was enough to make many citizens of the federation feel that they were about to have a mental breakdown, not to mention that they would be living in darkness for the rest of their lives!

In the video, encouraged by Jin Xinyue, Bing Bing finally summoned the courage to speak to billions of federal citizens.

She stammered and stammered. Sometimes, she even forgot what she was going to say. In the long silence, she simply sobbed. It was obvious that she had not received any good training.

But it was such sincere and simple words that hit every citizen of the federation like burning cannonballs.

“My—my name is Bingbing. I’m from the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Planet, almost twenty thousand meters below the ground.
