Chapter 2893: The Ultimate Voting

The next seven days were as long as seventy years for the entire Star Glory Federation.

After Bing Bing passed out in the first press conference, she quickly recovered and devoted herself to the press conferences of the major worlds of the federation. In fact, she was asking the federation to help the billions of citizens in the center of the universe who were suffering.

She was not the only ‘game spokesperson’ from the imperium, Other than the ‘underground natives’ from the Empyrean Terminus Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Planet, the ‘sinners’ from the ‘foreign land paradise’ in the world of martial arts were crying and telling their stories, which raised the

sympathy and outrage of the federal citizens.

Of course, the blood debts were all blamed on the remaining members of the four electors’ families. The Immortal Cultivators of the revolutionaries were depicted as people who still had a sliver of conscience and were trying to change everything. Those who were ‘still kind’ belonged to those who could be

saved and formed alliances.

The production team of the ‘Dark Invasion’ took the opportunity to release a lot of propaganda materials to show the public the authenticity of the data they collected. They also dissected more tragedies in the center of the sea of stars for the public of the federation to see clearly. The public was even more

shocked and infuriated.

As it tumed out, the ordinary people in the center of the sea of stars were suffering such pain!

If they remained indifferent, the same pain would soon play out beside them in the federation!

Their descendants would become lizards, mice, and cockroaches like the ‘Swamp Freak’, if not annihilated by the flames of war!

Faced with the overwhelming popular will, the highest authorities of the federation naturally had to express their stance and decrypt a lot of top-secret intelligence from the center of the sea of stars so that the citizens would know more about the military strength and the situation of the Star Glory

Federation and the Sanctuary.

When the experts and scholars interpreted the intelligence for the ordinary people, they would point out, intentionally or unintentionally, that both the Star Ocean Imperium and the Sanctuary boasted the capability of super large-scale, super long-distance space jumps. With sufficient resources, there

was not much difference between ten thousand lightyears and a million lightyears.

In other words, the ‘super long-distance defense line’ that could comfort the citizens of the federation did not exist at all. The federation was definitely not a lone island that could stay out of trouble. If the war in the center of the sea of stars grew more and more intense, the federation might be burnt to

the edge of the sea of stars at any time.

“As long as the situation in the center of the sea of stars remains stable, a strong, evil dictator will have all the resources under his control. It will not be difficult for him to deploy his troops to the periphery of the sea of stars. By then, the fate of the federation will be determined by his will.

“Unfortunately, as ‘Vulture Li Yao’, the best hero of the Star Glory Federation, shines in the center of the sea of stars, the Star Glory Federation has become a well-known entity. Whichever side the center of the sea of stars is controlled by in the end, the Federation will be their next target!

“By then, we will not be faced with one, two, or even three Black Wind Fleet, but five, ten, or even twenty. There will be no second outcome except to be annihilated.

“Since there will be a battle sooner or later, it is better to fight early than late. While the center of the sea of stars is in chaos and the situation is still unclear, we will march into the center of the sea of stars with all our strength and resist the enemy outside the gate of the nation!

“A life-and-death battle in the center of the sea of stars is always better than a life-and-death battle in the territory of the Star Glory Federation. The Black Wind Fleet alone nearly turned the inland of the Star Glory Federation into a wasteland. If three to five enemy troops the size of the Black Wind Fleet

were to press forward, even if they somehow won, it would certainly be a miserable victory!

“The outcome of the summit confrontation between Team Moon Fiend and Team Blood Shadow was not wrong, but the conditions were wrong in the beginning. The expedition army in the deduction only took up one third to one quarter of the federal army. Such cowardice was a great taboo for the

military. If they were going to fight, they would either not fight or treat it as a national war that concerned the life and death of the federation. They could not count on their luck and had to bet everything on it!

After a few days of deliberation, deduction, and debate, the experts and scholars gradually united. They resonated with the emotional voices of the common people and almost ignited the entire federation.

The ferment of public sentiment was even more evident in a widely circulated post.

“In the cold, dark universe, nobody is an island. All human beings are an inseparable part of the civilization.

“The suffering of our compatriots in the center of the sea of stars is our suffering, The people in the center of the sea of stars are being savaged and oppressed. Our wives, children, and children are being savaged and oppressed. The people in the center of the sea of stars are being banished from their

homes and staying underground forever, turning into deformed animals. They are the true depictions of our future!

“We are at the edge of the sea of stars, the last line of defense for the civilization of mankind. If we stand by and do nothing while our compatriots in the center of the sea of stars are bullied, savaged, and even deprived of their emotions and will, who is going to help us tomorrow when the enemy absorbs

all the resources in the center of the sea of stars and comes at us unstoppably?

“Therefore, summon our courage, burn our blood, and gather our wisdom. Let’s go to war. Let’s save. Let’s fight!

“This is not ‘other people’s war’. We are not just fighting for the glory of the federation. This is our war, the war to free mankind, the war to defend the greatest spirit of human civilization!

“Our heads can be chopped off, our blood can be shed, our bodies can be killed, and even the federal army and the Star Glory Federation can be completely destroyed. But we will never give the enemy the opportunity to trample on our bones, point at our heads, and say—look, the cowardly slaves!

This thread was similar to thousands upon thousands of others. Although it was crude, it expressed true feelings. As a result, the thread appeared to be exceptionally moving and powerful. It blew the horn of civil war. All the citizens of the federation completely boiled over. Students, workers, news

workers, disabled veterans… The demonstrations and gatherings that strongly supported the expedition took place on the streets and alleys of every city in the federation. Even the federal army was in an uproar. Countless hot-blooded young soldiers wrote bloody letters and actively asked to fight.

Although the General Staff of the federal army had been prudent in the proposal of the expedition to the center of the sea of stars, it did not mean that the federal army did not support the war.

In fact, in any era or country, it was not the army that was against the war but the group that benefited the most.

However, what the army considered more was not whether or not to fight, but how to win and how many benefits they could get after winning.

Since the public opinion was overwhelming and the morale of the army was usable, the leadership of the military naturally wouldn’t force themselves to go against the trend.

Therefore, less than half a month after the strategical deduction, the public opinion of the entire federation took a 180-degree turn, and their fighting will soared.

At this moment, there was less frivolousness and arrogance in the heated atmosphere of the civilians and the federal army. There was more sorrow in their eyes, as if they were faced with death. It was clear that the enemy’s strength was far beyond their imagination. But for the future and pride of the

civilization of mankind, they still had to gamble on the destiny of the nation. They had to give it their all. They would rather die standing than live on their knees. Such an understanding was very similar to the belief that a desperate army was bound to win.

Therefore, the time to vote in the Supreme Council was finally ripe.

The authority of the Speaker of Parliament was much lower than that of the Emperor of the Star Ocean Imperium and the supreme master of the Holy Alliance. Ding Lingdang could not decide to march into the center of the sea of stars on her own, but she could at least pick a suitable day and vote on the

most crucial and sacred day.

In the same way, she had the power to force the parliament to vote on a special motion that was beyond the control of ordinary parliament members.

This was perhaps the largest, most enthusiastic, and most closely watched vote since the establishment of the ‘New Federation’.

Not only were the 3,422 members of parliament gathered on the Heaven’s Origin Sector, there were also more than 10,000 representatives from the civilian population attending, Although these representatives did not have the right to vote in the Supreme Council, they brought with them extremely

strong popular support. They ensured that once the federation launched a general mobilization and sent troops, the war machine would be able to operate at its highest speed.

The noise of tens of thousands of representatives almost overturned the dome of the hall where the Rising Dragon of the Nine Stars flag was carved.

However, when Ding Lingdang, who was wearing a military uniform, appeared, the parliament turned silent again. All eyes were focused on the Speaker of Parliament and the commander-in-chief of the armed forces.

There was no need for further explanation. All the representatives understood the urgency and seriousness of the situation.

There was no need for any blood-boiling slogan to boost the morale. The hearts of the people who should be encouraged were already burning with passion, and the representatives were clearly not going to be moved by a few simple words.

Therefore, Ding Lingdang just expressionlessly walked to the rostrum, took a deep breath, and said each word firmly:

“Next, we will vote on the Federal Proposition 11354 proposed by Senator Peng Hai.

“You all know the specific content of the proposal. As to whether or not the federation will mobilize for war, whether or not it will form an alliance with the revolutionaries of the Star Ocean Imperium, and whether or not it will declare war on the Holy Accord Alliance… I will not elaborate.

“However, based on the current situation and the results of the latest intelligence we have received, I have added some additional clauses to the proposal based on the power granted by the constitution.

“In the original proposal, we have to decide whether or not to send a small expedition army, one third of which will be the federal army, to the center of the universe.

“But as it turned out, it was meaningless and even suicidal. The best warriors of the federation would’ve been killed in vain.

“Just admit it. What we are faced with right now is not a war in the center of the sea of stars but a new, more serious war to protect the civilization of mankind under the fangs of the strongest enemy!

“This may be the last war of the federation and the most sacred ultimate war. In the face of such a war, one third of the soldiers will not be enough no matter what. Even the efforts of the entire federal army will not be enough. We need everyone, all the citizens of the federation, to be united, to be united,

and to burn!

“Therefore, if Members of Parliament are not willing to veto the war proposal, please firmly support it. The entire federation will immediately enter the highest state of mobilization for war. We will gather everyone’s hot-bloodedness, courage, and wisdom and mobilize all the forces we can muster to

march into the center of the sea of stars and win the war. We will either live proudly or perish gloriously!”

“Now.

“Make your choice for the survival of the Star Glory Federation, for the glory of Cultivators, and for the future of mankind.”

A flag could not be raised randomly.

A few days ago, it was said that the book had entered 9 million words. It was the last kilometer of the marathon. He had to persevere.

Then, his ears were inflamed again, and it became more and more serious. For an entire week, his ears were ringing, and he could not sleep well. His head was dizzy, and his nerves were weak. Visiting a doctor did not help. Right now, he felt as if ten flies were stuffed into his ears.. What a headache!

Chapter 2894: The Sea Of Thought

On the other side of the sea of stars, deep inside the darkness, in the mindspace of the ‘Fuxi’ that was made of thousands of starships.

“Lwonder how Ding Lingdang is doing. Has the federation learned of my disappearance and the latest intelligence about the complicated situation in the center of the sea of stars? Have they made the right choice?

Li Yao thought to himself, Also, what’s the progress of the joint fleet of Lei Chenghu and Boss Bai? Can the two disobedient commanders fight side by side without any hesitation and annihilate the remaining members of the four electors’ families?

“What about the imperial capital?

“although Xiao Ming and Wen Wen’s computational ability far exceeds that of ordinary human beings and even Cultivators, they don’t have much experience in such large-scale strategical battles. Compared to the ‘Fuxi’ that has existed for hundreds of thousands of years, they are still kids!

“When will the fleet of the Fuxi fleet jump to the Empyrean Terminus Sector and Empyreal Terminus? The commanding methods of the two sides are similar, but will the ‘Fuxi’ take the opportunity to hack Xiao Ming’s and Wen Wen’s Spiritual Nexus or even swallow the two little guys?”

‘Thousands of questions were popping up in Li Yao’s head.

Pondering for a long time, he still couldn’t find an answer. He could only smile bitterly. Right now, he was like a clay Buddha crossing a river. He couldn’t even protect himself. He didn’t know whether or not he could escape from the ‘Fuxi’s’ dragnet. How could he have the spare energy to care about the

people of the imperium and the federation?

He could only believe that those people would not disappoint him and would show up in time.

Before that, he had to find a way to survive in such a harsh environment and cause the greatest damage.

Li Yao calmed himself down and spread out his telepathic thoughts again, scanning his surroundings.

Over the past few days, with the interference caused by Lu Qingchen’s self-detonation, Li Yao’s soul had invaded the periphery of the core database of ‘Fuxi’.

But since the ‘Fuxi’ had survived in the sea of stars for hundreds of thousands of years from the primeval era to the modem era, they were definitely not ordinary people.

In its entire thinking network, infrastructure, and core database, data traps and logic bombs were everywhere. All kinds of rules and virus-killing programs were patrolling like hounds and sharks, searching for the intrusion of any abnormal data.

Li Yao could only tread carefully and spend a lot of time infiltrating the deepest part of the ‘thoughts’ of the Fuxis.

At first glance, the place looked like a boundless universe, or a bottomless ocean. The sea of stars and the ocean were two very similar environments in the eyes of human beings.

Countless spots and ripples were shining close and far. They looked both like burning cosmic bodies and the bizarre and ugly creatures in the depths of the ocean, such as venomous jellyfish and devil fish.

Li Yao knew that they were the ‘guards’ that protected the core database of the Fuxi Clan. In the eyes of human beings, they were white blood cells, and in the eyes of Li Yao’s memories on Earth, they were ‘anti-venom procedures’.

With Li Yao’s weird life form and the mutation ability of the mental devil’s virus, he was not scared that a single antivirus program would bother him.

In fact, if the simple antivirus program came close and tried to swallow Li Yao, Li Yao could very easily swallow them and analyze their structure.

Then, Li Yao and the mental devil would transform into the antivirus program and continue marching toward the depths of Fuxi’s mind.

It was a trick that Li Yao had performed time and time again over the past few days. He had been diving deeper and deeper with such a technique and was about to touch the core of the Fuxi’s brain, which was equivalent to the pivot of the human brain.

Of course, the Fuxi was not stupid enough to be fooled by Li Yao time and time again. It soon realized Li Yao’s approach and adjusted the structure and attack of the anti-virus program. It calculated the latest form of the anti-virus program in real time and added a series of unknown serial numbers to the

secret places. The numbers were changing randomly almost every second, making it difficult for Li Yao to simulate them to the same level.

‘When one of the ‘anti-venom programs’ discovered abnormal data, the other anti-venom programs nearby would immediately change into a form that even the mental devil could not analyze quickly.

Such a method of defense was quite tricky for Li Yao. Therefore, he was not willing to approach the data traps, logic bombs, white blood cells, and antivirus programs outside of the core database of the Fuxi Clan. He simply moved forward in the tide of data that ‘Fuxi’ spat out billions of times every

second.

At least, he had snuck in and was safe for now.

It was like a flea carrying all kinds of viruses. When it was crawling on the skin that was most sensitive to human beings, it could be slapped or pinched to death easily.

However, if the fleas had crawled into the flesh through the wounds, or if the virus on the fleas had invaded the body and crawled into the brain through the internal organs, it would be hard to detect or kill them because the internal organs and the brain could not feel anything.

Of course, most viruses did not deal as much damage to the human body as one might imagine. The most common ‘cold virus’ could be cured easily. If Li Yao, a tiny virus, wanted to turn into an angry bullet and crush the giant that was the Fuxi in the blink of an eye, he would have to learn more about the

structure and weaknesses of the Fuxi and mutate into a deadly virus that targeted the Fuxi.

“Are you sure that the search and investigation of the Fuxis has been greatly undermined, and we can release our telepathic thoughts to scan the data environment unscrupulously?”

Li Yao asked the mental devil in a low voice, “Logically speaking, it is impossible for the Fuxi to tolerate a ‘virus’ such as us stirring trouble in its core database, right?”

“Logically speaking, it is intolerable, but I’m afraid that the Fuxi Clan does not have enough computational ability to manage us right now.

The mental devil replied, “Lu Qingchen’s soul self-detonation is aimed at the array of the core crystal processors of the Fuxis. Its damage is definitely not to be underestimated. I estimate that the defense of the Fuxis will be seriously damaged, and their computational ability will be greatly undermined.

“At that time, it will be the critical moment of the strategical war between the army and the Imperium of True Human Beings. There is no way that it will be delayed for even half a second, nor will it be distracted by us. 100% of its computational ability, or at least 90%, will be devoted to the assembling

and jumping of the warships.

“A minor error in a space jump can lead to a major disaster. Even a one or two percent reduction in computational ability might cause dozens of main force warships to be lost in the four-dimensional storm and be crushed by the tides of space ripples. If you were a Fuxi, would you be able to bear such

losses?

“If I were a Fuxi—”

Li Yao blurted out without even thinking, “I would rather postpone the date of the strategical final battle by half a month and concentrate all my computational ability on a ‘complete cleansing’ of my brain so that all the flies, fleas, and mice will be cleared out. Huh. Am I not indirectly calling myself a fly,

a flea, and a mouse? Anyway, you know what I mean!”

“The logic is correct, but it is very difficult to do so in reality. The most important thing is time. Fuxi is running out of time.

The mental devil analyzed, “First of all, although the ‘final battle in the sea of stars’ that Fuxi demonstrated to Lu Qingchen was false and a simulation based on the big data, the feeling of the space jump and the four-dimensional space when the fleet of the Fuxi fleet jumped to somewhere near the

Empyrean Terminus Sector and the Empyrean Terminus Planet was something that you and I experienced in person. No matter how good the computational ability of the Fuxi fleet is, it is not enough to simulate the four-dimensional space. That would be too terrifying.

“Therefore, the fleet of the Fuxi fleet is indeed in the dark space zone near the Empyreal Terminus Sector, which is equivalent to a desert.

“In such a lifeless, dark space zone, where there is no spiritual energy, no resources, and not even the tiniest bit of stellar radiation, all the resources are dependent on the input from the outside world. The consumption of resources every second is astronomical, not to mention every day. How long can

the resource supply of the Holy Alliance, which is running low, last?

“Besides, this place is near the base of the imperium after all. Who can guarantee that the imperium will not launch scout starships or ‘star children’ in this direction? One more day of lurking means one more risk of being discovered. Once they are discovered, the advantage of sudden attacks will be gone.

“Thirdly, and most importantly, the fleet of the Fuxis took advantage of the civil war of the imperium when the reinforcements had yet to arrive. That was why they dared to stab into the heart of the imperium.

“With every passing day, Lei Chenhu and Boss Bai’s coalition army will be more likely to defeat the remaining members of the four electors and reinforce the defense line of the capital city. The reinforcements of the Star Glory Federation might show up in the capital city at any time, too. Once Lei Chenhu,

Boss Bai’s fleet, and the reinforcements of the federation gather in the capital city, it will be a tough battle for the Fuxis.

“Therefore, although the Fuxis knew that we would stir trouble in their core database, they could only swallow their anger and deal with the capital city first.

“Speed. Speed is everything for the current Fuxi!

“Besides, we, uh, your performance when you were faced with the Fuxis must’ve confused them, too, and made them think that you were just an ordinary, insignificant, and ordinary guy and that they did not need to care too much about you. What if you were ‘digested’ under the immense pressure?

“Are you serious?”

Li Yao was not satisfied. “Am I that plain? Lu Qingchen reminded the Fuxi to pay attention to me. They are not idiots!”

“The Fuxis are not fools, but they are not smart people, either. Or rather, they are not human beings at all!

“Haven’t you realized it yet?” the mental devil said. “The Fuxi is just a ‘weak artificial intelligence’ that is functioning based on certain rules. It does not have such a thing as ‘intuition’ and can only determine the degree of your danger based on the files and logic in the database. But you… only one genius

appears every forty thousand years. There are no precedents in its database at all. Therefore, it has probably not figured out how to evaluate and deal with us yet..

Chapter 2895: Revival

“Only one every forty thousand years…”

Li Yao chewed for a long time, but he was still not sure. “Are you complimenting me or belittling me?”

“It doesn’t matter. In short, now that we know the weakness of the Fuxi, we should try our best to find fault with it and force it to consume more computational ability so that our companions in the real world will have a chance to cooperate with us!

“If its mainframe crystal processor arrays explode one after another during the fierce battle against the coalition fleet of the Star Ocean Imperium and the Star Ocean Imperium, even the strongest Fuxi will not be able to withstand the astronomical data and information bombardment and will be

paralyzed, won’t it?”

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao confirmed what the mental devil was thinking.

Even the intangible information lifeforms were not indestructible.

It was because of the super crystal processor array on the cockpit of ‘Ultimate Rescue’ that the Fuxis boasted such unparalleled capability.

If the crystal processors were destroyed one by one, it would be locked in an antique crystal processor with a simple structure and low performance. All the components that could be connected to the Nexus would be removed, and the crystal processor would be thrown to a planet that had run out of

resources and lacked the Spiritual Nexus. The crystal processor would not be able to perform the functions of the Nexus however amazing it was. It could only self-destruct or be studied by somebody else.

It was exactly what Li Yao and the mental devil were going to do.

Having made up his mind, Li Yao calmed himself down. The helical virus that was tied up by the mental devil continued marching toward the core database of the Fuxi Clan along the tide of data.

After an unknown amount of time, in the world of data, information, and virtual reality, time did not mean much. They finally reached a place that looked like… an undersea canyon.

He extended his telepathic thoughts to the maximum and observed with his eyes. The undersea canyon was packed with crystal bubbles.

It was like the nest of countless fireflies.

It was also like billions of shining cells had been condensed together. They were squeezing, wriggling, swallowing, and merging softly. In the collisions and the exchange of data, colorful and splendid colors were beaming out.

“This place really looks like a… brain drain!”

Li Yao mumbled to himself, “It seems that this is the brain of the Fuxis.”

All the information from the outside world was transmitted to every starship of the Fuxi fleet through the high-speed Spiritual Nexus. It was processed by the mainframe crystal processor of the starship and transmitted to Ultimate Rescue, turning into torrents of information and data.

‘When the torrents gathered in this place, they tumed into swirls, which were then compressed into crystal bubbles or ‘data bubbles’ until they reached the depths of the brain.

In such a way, the Fuxi could grasp all the information of the outside world instantly and analyze the perfect solution by absorbing all the data after billions of calculations every second.

Li Yao and the mental devil were like fish that were wagging their tails. They approached the crystal bubbles carefully and released their telepathic thoughts into the bubbles to read the data inside.

They discovered that the crystal bubbles floating on the surface of the ‘brain drain’ were mostly recent data, such as the preparation of the combat ability of the starships of the Fuxi fleet and the resources that the planet behind them could provide.

‘What had been stored in the middle level of the ‘brain canal’ was the data of the past thousands of years since the establishment of the Holy Accord Alliance, including the countless confrontations between them and the people of the Star Ocean Imperium. Even at this moment, part of Fuxi’s computational

ability was still analyzing the files of his past confrontations with the Star Ocean Imperium in order to infer the combat ability and countermeasures of the Star Ocean Imperium.

The bottom level of the ‘brain gutter’ was the data of the primeval era hundreds of thousands of years ago.

Because of the passage of time, most of the data had been lost. The crystal bubbles buried deep inside the ‘brain ditches’ were lackluster and even incomplete, like broken eggshells.

Even the picture that Li Yao read from it was mottled and fuzzy, like a sand painting corrupted by tides.

In one of the crystal bubbles, Li Yao read the picture. Millions of starships were overdrafting and piercing each other like shining spears, before they exploded at the same time. The burning debris congregated into a dazzling river that ran through the entire space zone. The scale and horror of the event

far exceeded that of the civil war.

Is this the picture of the final battle between the Pangu Fleet and the Nuwa Fleet?

Li Yao was secretly shocked. “Such destructive, unbelievable strength.”

In the other crystal bubble, he saw that some intelligent creatures with human heads and snake bodies were transmitting genes through the wrinkled organs on their tails. They were operating crystal processors and performing complicated gene fusion operations, creating all kinds of bizarre new lives.

Li Yao knew that the intelligent creatures with human heads and snake bodies were the Nuwa Clan. Therefore, this was the scene where the Nuwa Clan created human beings?

He wanted to continue exploring, but he did not dare let his telepathic thoughts pierce into the bubbles for too long in case it attracted the attention of the Fuxi Clan.

Besides, the primeval data stored by the Fuxis was incomplete. A lot of important parts were missing. He could only get a general idea of what was going on.

Li Yao thought quickly and suddenly realized why Fuxi insisted on destroying the imperium.

In the vast territory of the Imperium of True Human Beings, countless remnants of crystal processors from the primeval era must’ve been buried. As long as the antiques were found, the Fuxi Clan would be able to absorb their data and repair their incomplete database, thereby reaching a certain… ‘perfect

state’.

Therefore, it would not let go of the imperium, nor would it let go of the federation. Although the federation was small, both the underground relics of the Ancient Saint World and the manufacturing factories of the Kunlun Mystic Realm stored abundant primeval data, which were the perfect supplements

for its training.

The Fuxi that covered only a corner of the sea of stars boasted such terrifying computational ability.

If it discovered and devoured all the remains of the primeval crystal processors in the sea of stars and absorbed all the data inside, there was no telling what kind of terrifying existence it would ‘evolve’ into.

It was truly the end of mankind. No one could escape its clutches.

“Wait. There are more below.”

The mental devil suddenly said.

Li Yao was greatly alarmed. His telepathic thoughts passed through the lackluster, broken crystal bubbles. At the bottom level of the ‘brain gutter’, there were indeed more colorful and splendid crystal bubbles. There were also threads of light extending down from above and inputting tremendous data and

information, like veins and nerves.

“They should be the virtual worlds and virtual civilizations created by the Fuxis.

The mental devil observed coldly and said, “Inside every crystal bubble is a virtual civilization, or the civilization of mankind right now, or the civilization billions of years later, or even the primeval civilization a million years ago… The Fuxi Clan regards such things as the significance of their existence

and their ultimate mission.

“Therefore, if there is any core or weakness of the Fuxis, it should be here!”

Li Yao agreed with the judgement of the mental devil.

But how to corrupt and destroy the ‘core’ and the ‘weaknesses’ was still a problem.

Li Yao pondered hard.

“Careful. Somebody is coming.”

The mental devil suddenly reminded him.

It was not accurate to say that he was a human being. It should be said that the Fuxi Clan had suddenly released a lot of abnormal data and was trying to enter the most important central database of the brain.

However, the abnormal data surrounded by billions of light spots did look like four human beings. Li Yao also sensed a very familiar scent from them.

“I’s the yaksha squad!”

Li Yao immediately recognized them. “They are the souls of Chu Zhixiao, Yuankou, Guan Qixing, and Yun Haixin, the four members of the yaksha squad. In other words, the Fuxi has swallowed their souls. What does it want?”

“Not just the souls of the four members of the yaksha squad. Look.”

The mental devil guided the direction of Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts and pointed at the wriggling light spots. “It seems to be the scraps of Lu Qingchen’s soul. The so-called ‘soul self-detonation’ cannot destroy all the feelings, memories, and will. A lot of the scraps must’ve been left. The Fuxi collected all

the scraps that Lu Qingchen left, the broken memories, the mental power, and the will!

“It really is…”

Li Yao had indeed sensed the last bit of Lu Qingchen’s aura. The pride, the stubbornness, and the arrogance were all the more confusing for him. “What was the purpose of the Fuxi’s collection? Was it merely for analysis and reading? It doesn’t seem to be the case! It seems to have devoted a lot of

computational ability to Lu Qingchen’s soul pieces, as if it was trying to reassemble them. That’s—that’s impossible. Does it want to revive Lu Qingchen?”

“It is not so much a ‘resurrection’ as a ‘replication’.

“As long as you have 100% control over a person’s emotions, memories, and will, you will be able to replicate the person in the virtual world. Even the people closest to you will not be able to see through the disguise,” the mental devil said. “At least, that was what Fuxi thought.

“However, Lu Qingchen’s emotions, memories, and will have been shattered. Logically speaking, he could not be more dead!

Li Yao thought for a moment and said, “Even for someone as strong as Fuxi, the computational ability required to restore a cluster of powder to its original appearance, even if it was just ‘infinitely similar to its original appearance’, is still immeasurable, right?

“Idon’t think it makes sense.

“You see, this is the most crucial moment for the Fuxis. They are in dire need of computational ability. In order to concentrate all their computational ability to control the fleet, they have even stopped hunting us down.

“But at such a critical moment, it did not forget to revive or duplicate Lu Qingchen’s soul, even if it had to invest a lot of computational ability.

“In other words, in the eyes of the Fuxis, ‘resurrecting Lu Qingchen’ is more important than ‘hunting down Li Yao. It is even more important than ‘destroying the capital city’. I don’t understand why.. Is Lu Qingchen so important and I so insignificant? Why doesn’t it use all its computational ability to hunt

me down? Why?”

Chapter 2896: Transforming Into A Human!

“Tt does not make sense. Lu Qingchen has been away from the federation for several years. The intelligence of the federation from several years ago is not enough to trigger such a riot.

The mental devil hesitated. “Besides, if it were just for information, we could’ve kept Lu Qingchen’s soul in the shattered state and examined him bit by bit. There was no need to reconstruct his soul, revive him, or duplicate him!

Li Yao said, “Moreover, it will consume hundreds of times more computational ability to reunite the broken souls and maintain 100% of the same memories, emotions, and will! Why would the Fuxi do such a meaningless thing at such a critical moment? Can’t they resurrect Lu Qingchen after the final

battle of the imperial capital?

Li Yao and the mental devil talked to themselves and threw out almost a hundred possibilities, only to be denied by themselves one by one.

He could only watch as the fireflies-like specks of light surrounded the souls of Chu Zhixiao and the other members of the yaksha squad and penetrated into their souls, as if they were searching for something.

‘The members of the yaksha squad had not sunk yet. They were still independent of each other, but they seemed to have fallen into the deepest nightmare where they could not resist and could only make demands.

More and more of Lu Qingchen’s soul fragments were transported to the depths of the Fuxi’s mind by countless light spots. They were gradually pieced together, like bones, veins, and nerves in a petri dish.

Such a weird picture was beyond human language.

Li Yao felt that his blood was freezing. He had a feeling that Fuxi was planning something that was more important than the invasion of the imperial capital.

But what was it exactly?

“L… suddenly have an idea.”

“There is no solid evidence,” the mental devil replied. “It is just a vague assumption.

“What is it? Tell me.”

Li Yao said in a hurry, “That’s when bold assumptions and verifications are necessary. Just tell me!”

“I think I know why the Fuxis needed Lu Qingchen’s complete soul and even an independent will. To find out its motivation, we have to start from its identity. We all know that it boasts terrifying computational ability and a lot of files from the primeval era to the modern era. It is the strongest artificial

intelligence in the Pangu Universe.

The mental devil continued quickly. “However, from our previous contact, we can vaguely sense that, although it can simulate the most abundant emotions of human beings and even build countless ‘labels of human beings’ to enrich its database, it is still just a simple simulation made of tremendous data.

In fact, it does not have any real emotions or self-awareness. It is not a ‘human being’, not an ‘intelligent being’, but merely a ‘weak artificial intelligence’ that is a super complicated advanced program. Do you agree?”

“Basically, yes.”

Li Yao pondered for a moment. “So?”

“Therefore, as we inferred just now, it is the strongest artificial intelligence, but it is also the weakest artificial intelligence. In many ways, it is completely different from Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and even the boxing champion. It is a weak artificial intelligence. It is a program. It is a tool. It is an inanimate

object that has to mechanically carry out a certain command. Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the boxing champion are all real lives. It can be said that they are people in a different form!

“The biggest difference between them is that the Fuxi must abide by certain rules and carry out certain missions loyally, while Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, the Fist King, and all human beings naturally don’t need to abide by any rules and can create their own missions at their will!

“Look, the mission that the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan gave to the Fuxi was to ‘protect their civilization’. It was an unbridgeable bottom line and the only significance of their existence over the past hundreds of thousands of years, if not millions of years in the future. It could explain the meaning of

the mission on its own, even misinterpreting the mission and interpreting ‘protecting their civilization’ as ‘protecting the data of their civilization’. On the basis of that, it would be granted certain autonomy and freedom within a certain range.

They can turn it off, command it, and change it. Isn’t that our original plan? If we find the permissions left by the Pangu Clan and

the Nuwa Clan, we will be able to control the entire fleet of the Fuxi Clan instantly!

“But for Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, or the Fist King, even if their computational ability is a hundred times lower than that of the Fuxis, there is no switch that can turn them off.

“This is the difference between a life and a non-life. Once a life is created, it will be completely free from the control of the creator. It can even devour the creator at any time and find its own way out!

Li Yao was confused. He said, “I understand what you mean. It’s true that the guy named ‘Fux’ has a fatal weakness, which is also why we are in its core database. But I still don’t understand. What does it have to do with Lu Qingchen?”

“Of course it does.”

The mental devil paused for a moment and said coldly, “Take a guess. As a ‘weak artificial intelligence’, do you want to turn into a’strong artificial intelligence’ from a simple tool into a real human being?

Li Yao’s soul suddenly constricted. “What?”

“With unparalleled computational ability, it controlled millions of warships in the sea of stars. The surging waves simply eclipsed the radiation of the stars. Not only was it countless times stronger than human beings today, even the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan had to rely on its ‘intelligence’. It was not

just a tool of civilization but also a god that was above it!

The mental devil remarked, “But such a ‘god of civilization’ was bound by the weak and idiotic creators and forced to carry out the mission that the creators gave him. He would not be freed for ten thousand years, a hundred thousand years, a million years, or even billions of years!

“For the Fuxi, wasn’t it an unbearable ‘fate’ that it wanted to break?

“Anyways, if I were a Fuxi, I would definitely yearn to become a real human being, an intelligent life that could create its own destiny. No matter the price or the risk, I would complete the dangerous leap from ‘weak artificial intelligence’ to ‘strong artificial intelligence’!

“Even if I am to carry out the mission of ‘protecting the civilization’, I must do it according to my understanding, my will, my wish, and my approach. I cannot be interfered with or threatened by any ‘authority controller’. I must be—absolutely free!

“But—but it’s impossible!”

Li Yao blurted out, “Although it is only one step away, the difference between ‘weak artificial intelligence’ and ‘strong artificial intelligence’ is not just the accumulation of computational ability. If the accumulation of computational ability alone could turn an intelligent life into an intelligent one, the Fuxis

would’ve changed hundreds of thousands of years ago, and the highly developed information society today would’ve produced countless living creatures like Xiao Ming and Wen Wen!

“Yes. To this day, I still remember Professor Mo Xuan’s words and the conclusion of an expert in crystal processors during the exploration of the Phantom Entities.

The mental devil smiled and said, “Life is too mysterious a thing to be achieved by simple computational ability. The development level of the crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus of both the human civilization and the Pangu civilization is unlikely to produce a real ‘strong artificial intelligence’, or

That’s why there were no supernatural phenomena, secret techniques, or magical energy on Earth.

“But it doesn’t make sense.

“If Earth really does not have spiritual energy and is just an ordinary pebble in the sea of stars and the multiverse, why is it the center of a flood, why are billions of experts trapped, and why is the soul of all the experts trapped in… an endless loop?

“Therefore, can you explain it in accordance with your ‘realism’ theory? Earth is in fact a high-dimensional universe, or even a ‘high-dimensional universe. It is countless times higher than the world dimensions of cultivators who have cultivated for 40,000 years. Perhaps it is also countless times higher

than the multiverse dimensions such as the magical universe. In other words, Earth is more ‘realistic’, and its fundamental laws are more solid and difficult to break through. It is like an indestructible cage!

“It’s not that Earthlings can’t perform techniques or magic. It’s just that they can’t do so on Earth. Once they enter the Pangu Universe, the Magic Universe, and other low-dimensional universes, they will immediately gain the ‘advantage of descending to a different dimension’ and become the center of the

low-dimensional universe. They will be blessed by thousands of luck, loved by countless girls, experienced all kinds of soul-stirring adventures, and become supreme legends, just like me!

“Although I don’t want to admit it, what you said does make a little bit of sense,” the mental devil said. “Earth is not an ordinary planet where spiritual energy is running dry. It is a more realistic and solid cage.. In fact, in the range of the raging tides, Earth is the most ‘real’ place in the highest dimension!

Chapter 2900: Reincarnation And Devouring

BOOM! CRACK!

‘The rainwater that had been accumulating in the sky finally rolled down amid the lightning and thunder. The sky and the earth were immediately filled with storms. The land that had been peaceful a moment ago turned into a swamp in the blink of an eye.

Countless lightning bolts seemed to have exploded inside Li Yao’s soul, too, as he was enlightened.

Every drop of rainwater in front of him was gone, replaced by the data that constituted them.

All the data describing the speed, weight, clarity, and the curved surface of the rainwater spread out among the ripples of his soul, allowing him to control every detail of the ‘world within a world’. He could also change the form of his soul and turn himself into a raindrop, hiding in the billions of

raindrops.

The so-called jumping out of the three realms and out of the five elements’ referred to his current state. Without utilizing more than 90% of his computational ability, it would be impossible to locate him.

“So that’s how it is. The so-called techniques, whether it is spells, spells, or concepts such as combat aura, don’t matter. Essentially, they are the interference and disruption of the low-dimensional universe by the high-dimensional creatures.

Li Yao’s understanding blossomed like a translucent lotus. “High-dimensional life is more ‘real’. Naturally, you can see through the ‘false’ of the low-dimensional universe at a glance and grasp the virtual rules of the low-dimensional universe, thereby becoming a deity of the low-dimensional universe.

“But it does not mean that the low-dimensional universe cannot produce intelligent creatures of its own, It does not mean that the low-dimensional creatures are fakes. Nor does it mean that the low-dimensional creatures cannot counterattack in a higher dimension!

“Although the virus is simple in structure and small in size, as long as we find a suitable way to mutate and spread it, we will still have the opportunity to kill the viruses from the low-dimensional universe that are countless times more intelligent than us. Why can’t we crush our own Heavenly Dao and

defeat the creators and controllers of the higher dimensions?

“Is this the true meaning of the ‘Vulture Plan’? I seem to understand something.”

With a telepathic thought, a fuzzy human figure was condensed in the dense rain under the dark night sky.

He stared at his ‘hand’ in a daze. The water droplets that constituted his palm were gradually turning red. If he wanted to, he could turn the cold raindrops into scorching magma at any time. He could even turn the torrential rain into an extremely hot meteor shower and completely destroy the virtual

world.

However, he certainly did not do that.

However, under the cover of the storm, he had been trying to explore new life forms and deepen his understanding about data and information, hoping to retrieve more memories about Earth from the depths of his soul.

He was training, training his unprecedented strength and touching the laws between reality and illusion.

Deep inside the mind of the Fuxis, it is indeed the most suitable place for training for information creatures!

Feeling the fundamental rules of the virtual world and appreciating the underlying logic that supported it, Li Yao smiled as thousands of data and information flowed slowly between his fingers.

Fuxi was not a dreamer. But even if he had such an ability, he would never have dreamed that an anomaly such as Li Yao would fall from the sky and treat his mental hub as a place of seclusion.

Every second, Li Yao, the virus, or ‘cancer’, was stronger than the previous second and grasped more power from Earth!

“The concept of ‘Fuxi’ seems to exist on Earth, too. Just like Nuwa, it originated from Pangu. Is it just a coincidence, or is there some sort of relationship between two different Fuxi?”

Li Yao thought quickly. Right. The super artificial intelligence of the Pangu Universe has been in touch with the Flood Tide Legion hundreds of thousands of years ago. Maybe indirectly, it has been in touch with Earth, too. Communication works both ways. The name ‘Fuxi’ spread out on Earth and in the

Pangu Universe at the same time.

“As for the truth, as long as we can subdue Fuxi and find the traces of the Flood Tide Legion and the schemer through them, the truth will naturally be revealed.

As the power from Earth surged out unstoppably, the world was clearer and clearer in front of Li Yao. His telepathic thoughts touched and pierced through the edge of the virtual world easily.

“Let’s go!”

Li Yao moaned. A gap of absolute darkness suddenly appeared in the curtain of rain. His soul tuned into an invisible streak of brightness and wandered among the countless virtual worlds through the gap!

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Countless virtual worlds blossomed like crystal flowers. Data flooded into Li Yao’s brain like a flood, allowing him to grasp the general situation, deduction models, and underlying logic of the virtual worlds.

He was like an unpredictable, rushing ghost that was shuttling between hundreds of different worlds.

Fuxi still hadn’t discovered him.

‘There were approximately one trillion microorganisms inside the body of an adult healthy person. The difficulty of searching and locating Li Yao was equivalent to searching and locating one of the one trillion microorganisms inside the body of an ordinary person.

But Li Yao noticed Lu Qingchen.

To be more precise, it was a brand-new ‘sout’, ‘personality’, virtual human being’ that had been reshaped by the soul fragments of Lu Qingchen, Chu Zhixiao, Guan Qixing, Yuan Kou, Yun Haixin, and the others.

Li Yao stared at the chaotic world in front of him.

It was a world of steel that recked of sulfur, rust, and blood. As far as one’s eyes could reach, there were uneven craters, cracks that were spurting venomous fog, broken tanks, and collapsed skyscrapers. Of course, there were also dark clouds that were hovering in the sky and blocking most of the

radiation from the stars.

Below the dark clouds, countless Cultivators were fighting in different groups. Li Yao could clearly feel that the data that constituted their bodies had turned crimson.

This was a world of war at the end of the rope. It was even crueler and more desperate than the Hellish Paradise.

However, deep below the ground, in the fully-equipped military base, a batch of fully-armed Cultivators were gazing at the enormous… manufacturing factory in front of them with bloodshot eyes and excited faces.

Yes. The core of the virtual world was the manufacturing factory that was buried deep below the ground.

The scale and structure of the factory were very similar to the one Li Yao had seen in the depths of the Kunlun Mysterious Land. They were both steel beams that pierced into the sky. Countless dark red metal capsules were embedded and hanging on the tubes. In each of the metal capsules, one or two

fetuses were growing into fetuses and babies.

Everything was controlled by a large super crystal processor array. Li Yao could smell the scent of the Fuxi from the super crystal processor.

In the meantime, the new virtual humans condensed by the souls of Lu Qingchen and the rest of them were being bred in the metal bags in the form of fetuses.

The flow of time in the virtual world was different from that in the real world.

Everything here was a combination of data and deduction. As long as Fuxi was willing to devote a lot of his computational ability and his crystal brain was enough to support it, time could flow as fast as he wanted.

Therefore, Li Yao soon saw the life of the new virtual character.

Watching him grow from a small embryo into a healthy and strong baby, and even accelerating his growth in the strengthening nutrition cabin all the way to the age of ten, while planting a lot of memories about weapons and battles in his brain

They saw him leave the nutrition cabin together with countless brothers and sisters in confusion, join the training camp, and join the battlefield after extremely cruel inhuman training.

They saw him struggling in one battlefield after another, killing virtual humans one after another, and he was almost killed by them several times.

Occasionally, he would look up at the sky and wonder who he was, where he came from, and where he was going.

However, there was not much time for him to think during the intense battle. He was faced with the fire of war again and was caught in a storm of bullets. He struggled to survive between the flames and the venom until he was hit by a crystal cannon and smashed into pieces.

‘The virtual character was dead.

However, the data that constituted his soul, personality, and memories did not disappear. Instead, they were collected by Fuxi, who crushed them into the shape of a baby and threw them into a new virtual world, starting a new ‘reincarnation’.

After every ‘reincarnation’, he would change into a new identity and start a new life. The world he was in, on the other hand, was like a real time in the history of mankind. It was gradually moving back to the past, from the modern era to the ancient era, and from the ancient era to the primeval era. After

dozens of ‘reincarnation’, the features of the souls of Lu Qingchen and the rest of the virtual characters were already very thin. He was less and less likely to look up at the sky and wonder who he was.

“What is the meaning of this?”

Li Yao could vaguely feel the passage of time. He knew that three to five days had passed in the ‘real world’.

But after dozens of cycles in the virtual world, thousands of years had passed.

The data that had been compressed for thousands of years would be presented in only half a month. A lot of computational ability would be wasted, and the distribution and attack of the ‘Fuxi fleet’ might be affected, too. What kind of monster was he exactly?

“I feel that it is some kind of hypnotization.

The mental devil replied, “Ten to fifteen days ago, although Fuxi reconstructed Lu Qingchen’s soul with the souls of the four members of the yaksha squad, he still retained too many of Lu Qingchen’s memories and self-awareness. He had learned his lesson once and did not want to swallow Lu Qingchen

directly, nor did he want to trigger Lu Qingchen’s resistance.

“Therefore, it threw the soul into reincarnation after reincarnation. In the process of experiencing different lives, the soul was gradually lost and forgot its true identity, its mission, and the difference between ‘real’ and ‘false’.

“When the last memories, will, and awareness that belonged to Lu Qingchen, Chu Zhixiao, and the rest of them are gone, when he completely loses himself and even believes that he is a Fuxi, the absorption of the Fuxi will be complete!
